# II. Ethiopic version

**Author**: Timothée Ælure  
**Series**: PO Tome 49  
**Columns**: pp. 200–280  

[p. 200] {#po.49.p200}
ዘከመ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ዮስቋም ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አምላክ ።
How our Lord stretched out His hand upon a rock at the mountain of Qosqâm (Quesquäm). In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
ድርሳን ፡ መናዝዝ ፡ ወፍሥሐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዜና ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘትእምርት ። ወዜና ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ፤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ሞስቋም ። በረከቱ ፡ የሃሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ 'ለዓለመ ፣ ዓለም፤።
(Arabic 87) Homily of consolation and joy on the account of the consecration of the church of the Mark and (on) the account of the manner in which our Lord Jesus Christ stretched out His hand upon a rock at the mountain of Qosqâm. May His blessing be with us for ever and ever.
ይቤ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወልደ ፤ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ዘተፃብዓ ፡ በአሰሮሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡፤ በቅድመ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ኬልቄዶናዊ ፡ ርኩስ ። ወዘአስተርከበት ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መርቅያን ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ። ወኀደጋ ፡ ለብርክያል" ፣ ንግሥት ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍሬ ፤ ወሠረዎ ፡ ለዮናልዮስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዕመቀ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ወለዩስትናኖስኒ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማዕምቅ ፡ ወአጥፍኦ ፡ ለልዮን ፡ ወታአውጣርይጡስ ፡ ወሚክያኑስ ፡ ዘፀረፈ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ አርቶዶክሳዊያን ፤ ወዘተዓገሠ ፣ ድካማቲሁ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡፣ ዘተወክፎን ፡ እምኔሆሙ“ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አመ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአብዓለ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ወጸጋ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ከለለቶ ፡ ለዕፀ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ልህቃኑ ። ወነሥአ ፡ መንበረ ፡ እለ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፤ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ስደቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ዘንተ ፡ድርሳነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በአዝማን ፡ ዘሖረ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ፋው ፡ዘበስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እልአርሴ ፡ መትርያድስ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ አበ ፡ ደርገት ። ወይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ድርሳነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሐዋርያነ" ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ድርሳነ ፡ ውሔደ ፡፣ እምክብራተ ፡ ሊቅት ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ። ወበእንተ ፡ ዕርገተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ አመ ፡ ናእሱ ፡ አመ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ነጊዶቶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ በላዕሌሃ ፡ ስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡
The holy father Timothy (Timotëwos) says, (he) the patriarch of Alexandria, the (spiritual) son of the father patriarch the holy Dioscorus (Diyosqoros) who fought in the footsteps of the apostles before the impious and impure council of Chalcedon, whose prayer drew death upon Marcian (Margeyän) before (the term fixed for) his days, (who) left the empress Pulcheria (Berkeyäl) without fruit, annihilated Juvenal (Yonäleyos) in the depths of hell, also sent Justinian (Yustenänos) into the abyss, destroyed Leo (Leyon), Theodoret (Tä'awtäryetus), and Mikeyänus who had blasphemed the faith of those of the right faith, and who bore with constancy his many trials that he endured at their hands, (he) this father, when they exiled him, he who is the father Dioscorus the holy. Then he celebrated a feast with the angels in the land of (eternal) life; indeed, the spirit and the grace that were in him crowned the balsam, the holy Timothy. (The latter) was for him his own son in the time of his old age and he received the see of Alexandria after the death of the holy father Dioscorus in his exile. He pronounced this homily in the land of Egypt at the moment when he went to the consecration of the church of Fâou (Fäw) which (is) in the name of the holy father the archimandrite Pachomius (Päkuemis), the father of cenobitism. He pronounced this homily while bishops were with him, and numerous were those who had come with him. He pronounced this homily - (made of) some praises of the mistress Our Lady Mary the pure Virgin and (which treats) of the ascent of our Lord Christ into the land of Egypt during His childhood - when they arrived, at the moment when they (= Timothy and his retinue) were traveling, at this rock to which has been given the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, only-begotten Son of God. This mark occurred because of His coming

ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዋሕድ ። ወኮነት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ነጊዶቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ፅዒድ ፡ ወተሰምየት ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እስመ ፡ እደ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኅቱም ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ዘእምእግዚእ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ተሃሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
into Egypt at the moment when He was traveling toward the district of the South; this rock was called "extension of the hand of our Lord," for the hand of the Lord Christ is imprinted there - in the peace that (comes) from the Lord. May His holy blessing be with His servant our king David for ever and ever, amen.

### Chapter 1
ክፍል ፣፤ 8። አማን ፡ ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝክራቲሆሙ ፡ ለንጹሓን ፡ እስመ ፡ እምከመ ፡ ተሐውሰት ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ወፄናሆሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ናሁ ፡ ይመልእ ፡ ዝክተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ ርጌታተ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ለዝኩ ፡ ሕፃን ። ወይትፌሥሑ፤ ፡ አልባበ ፡ መፍቀርያነ ፡ ትምህርት ፡ ወዕፍረተ ፤ ርጌታቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ክብራተ ። ናሁ ፡ መልአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገዳማተ ፡ ወበድዋተ ፡ እስከነ ፡ ዮርዳኖስኒ ፡ ዘተፈጸመ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ጥምቀቱ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወአዋከየ ፡ ወፄነወ ፡ ዕፍረተ ፣ ርጌታቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወአውግር ፡ ዘወፅአት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ አጽናፍ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤፍራጥስኒ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ። ወፄነወ ፡ ዕፍረቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአፄነወ ፡ ዝክተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ 8፳ ወርእየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተቀበለት ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወእዴሁ ፡ ዘተተክለት ፡ ዲቤሁ ። ወባረከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አድባራተ ፡ ወማያተ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሑረቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፅዒድ ፡ ዘግብጽ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ኃብረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ባሕል ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ እንዘ" ፡ ይብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ በግብጽ ፡ ወመንክራቲሁ ፡ በመዛርዓ ፡ ሕፃን ። ናሁ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ጸንዓ ፣ ሰጊድሂ ፤ ወውዱሳት ፡ መካናቲሃ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡፤ በእንተ ፤ ምጽአተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወጥያቄ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አዕርፎቱ ፡ ውስቴታ ።
"In truth, my friends, beautiful is the memory of the pure, for, after their history and their good odor have circulated in (every) place, behold they fill this entire place with fragrances of perfume for this child (= Christ). They rejoice, the hearts of the friends of the teaching and of the perfume of the fragrances of the Lord which is greater than all honors. Behold (this memory) has filled all the deserts and the solitudes even to the Jordan itself, in which was accomplished the joy which (is) concerning the baptism of the Lord Christ in it (= the Jordan). The perfume of the fragrances of the Lord Christ shines and embalms upon the mountains and the hills, He who spreads to the extreme ends (of the earth?) and even to the river Euphrates, the great watercourse. His (= Christ's) perfume embalms because of the Lord Christ. Everyone breathes it and sees His grace, principally this rock which received the name of our Lord and His hand which was applied upon it. He (= Christ) blessed all the mountains and the waters at the moment of His coming into the South of Egypt. Because of that, behold it agrees with this statement, the word of David saying concerning this rock: He set His signs in Egypt and (did) His wonders by the arms of a little child. Behold the wonders of the Lord are firmly established as well as the adoration, the praises (and) their (= the wonders') places. This mark (is) concerning the coming of God the Word into the region of Egypt and (it is) a proof of His rest in it."

### Chapter 2
ክፍል ፤ ፪። ወለእመ ፡፣ አፍቀርከ ፡ ኦፍቁር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሑር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወትስግድ ፡ ለመካነ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ወትትመጠው ፡ በረከታ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ አእምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ስግደት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኩለሄ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ አልብከ ፡ ኑዛዜ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ መድኅን ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካነ ፡
If you desire, friend, to go to Jerusalem to prostrate yourself at the place of the worship of the Lord Christ and receive its (= the place's) blessing, then know that prostration is for the Lord Christ everywhere. But in Jerusalem, there is no consolation for you because of the word of the Savior, for it is the place of his

Chapter 2 If you desire, friend, to go to Jerusalem to prostrate yourself at the place of the adoration of the Lord Christ and receive its (= the place's) blessing, then know that the prostration is for the Lord Christ everywhere. But in Jerusalem, there is no consolation for you because of the word of the Savior, for it is the place of His vivifying passion. If you look toward the dwelling of the high priest, you will remember that soldier who slapped our Lord Jesus Christ in the face. Because of that therefore, tears befit you there. If you see the dwelling of Pilate there where they scourged the Lord Jesus Christ, at that moment strike your breast. If you see Golgotha and (even) if you do not suffer, grieve therefore for it is the place of the passion of the Lord Christ. Because of that therefore, weep and grieve. Above all, if you see the tomb of the Lord Christ, remember the affliction of His mother Our Lady the Virgin Mary, for in this place she wept. Jerusalem therefore is completely the place of rest of the humility of the Lord Christ and the city of the Hebrews is the place of His sufferings. As for the land of Egypt, it is the place of the joy of the Lord Christ. There are recited to you the psalms and the glorifications in all places and in the houses and the churches of Egypt. Consider the return of our Lord the merciful Christ into Egypt, as David says: || He assigned the mountains to the chamois and the rocks as shelters to the conies || He came to them, He loved their (= the Egyptians') humility and their weakness and He dwelt in the mountains. To the strangers He gave grace because He had dwelt in the deserts to the point of being a consolation for the wild animals. As a man whom He created, our Lord Christ went up to the rock (This) was a sign for the ascent onto the cross; indeed, He went up there and drew everyone to Him. That is to say that the Lord of the heights came, humbled Himself for us and recalled the lowering of those who (were) in hell: before the coming of the Lord Christ, hell was honored and the land of Egypt was fearsome. But when the Lord Christ dwelt there, He suppressed the curse; it found grace and it was praised more than all peoples. As for hell, when the Lord Christ descended into it, it was glorified because of that and praised because of His (= Christ's) descent into it. When He brought up from it the one He had chosen, He brought up with Him many souls to paradise. In return, hell wrapped itself in impurity and reproach far from the One who had dwelt in it. After that, no one will ever visit it. The land of Egypt was previously (considered) in poverty by all. It was henceforth praised because of the sojourn in it of the Lord Christ. Hell was famous when Adam was cast into it; henceforth, because of the Lord Christ, hell was poor after having been filled with the race of Adam and his descendants.
ሶበ ፡ ተወርወ ፡ 'ውስቴቱ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወይእዜሰ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ነድየ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ እምትዝምደ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ። ክፍል፡ዮ። አማን ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ሐዋዝ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ። ወዝኩ ፡ ተዝካር ፡ ዘኢየኃልፍ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ጌር ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ በውስተ ፡
Chapter 3 (Arabic 89, 169) In truth, obviously delectable (is) the memory of the righteous, like the memory of our holy father Pachomius. This memory which will not pass (is) rich, excellent and beautiful in the midst of the monks who (are) filled with speech (made) of the glorification of God night and day and completely. This took place thus: (there is) the father of the monks and head of the monasteries of Fâou, the pure elder abbä Pachomius (Päkum) for the consecration of whose church we were sent. While we (were) in Alexandria before meeting the grace of this place and before its light shone for us, and while we were sent into the monastery of these monks to receive their blessing, behold it happened that we were sent and we arrived at the community of the angels, guardians of the palace of their Lord. We came from Alexandria because of the (spiritual) labor of the elder monk and his children. We found on our path a solid foundation upon a rock. Behold a wise man has made for us a solid and beautiful construction upon the rock in truth. We came from Alexandria because of the cells of the monks similar to the angels by their hymns and their glorifications (addressed) to God. By the grace of God, (we came) by the way of the river until we had arrived at the memorial of the coming of our Savior by whom we were liberated. || He gave to the monks the good of abstinence and filled them with the power of the Holy Spirit. He made happy their glorifications to God the Father and to the Lord Jesus Christ who strengthened the mountains, (He) who dominates the abysses by His mysteries, who draws the light of the stars to Him, who guides the sun so that it lights the day and the moon so that it illuminates the night and who leads the fruits to term, He who made the Jordan recede when it saw Him, who went up upon the rivers of Egypt, who strengthened Peter upon the rock. || He revealed to him His glory and said to him: "Upon this rock, I will build my Church and the gates of hell will not prevail against it." And again concerning this rock of blessing: it is the one (upon) which He established the covenant with Peter, He came and sojourned upon it. This rock is higher than the two stones which (are) outside the altar, in which Jeremiah the prophet deposited the vessels of the altar and the keys until the moment when he would have returned from the captivity of Babylon. Daniel also praised a rock and said: "I, I saw a rock and behold a shard detached from it without the hand of a man and it did not damage this rock from which it detached." Our Lord Jesus Christ also praised a rock and said: "Whoever hears my word and keeps it resembles the wise man who built his house upon a rock. When the torrents flowed and the winds blew and attacked this house, it did not fall, for it is founded upon a rock."

ማእከሎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ዘበአስኬማሁ ፡ መልአካዊ ፡ አምሳሊሆሙ ፡ ለመላእክት ። ወደቂቁኒ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ምሉኣን ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ ወፍጹመ ፡ ውእቱኬ ፡ አበ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወሊቀ ፡ ምኔታት ፡ ዘፋው ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ 'አባ ፡ ጳኩም፤ ፡ ዘተፈኖነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወንሕነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፤ ለዝመካን ፣ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ያብርህ ፡ ለነ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ፍንዋን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ለነሚአ ፡ በረከቶሙ ፡፥ ናሁ ፡ ደደቀ ፡ ተፈንዎትነ ፡ ወበጻሕነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዓቃብያን ፡ ለታዕካተ ፡ እግዚኦሙ ። ወመጻእነ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ እምእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፃማሁ ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወደቂቁ ። ወረከብነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖትነ ፡ መሠረተ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። ናሁ ፡ ገብረ ፡ለነ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ ሕኑዎ ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ እምእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ በእንተ ፡ በአታቲሆሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡፣ መሳልያን ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በዘምሮቶሙ ፡፤ ወሰብሖቶሙ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወበጸጋሁ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍናዌ ፡ ባሕር ፡ እስከ ፡ በጻሕነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለመድኃኒነ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ተባላህነ ። ወወሀበ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘትኅርምት ፡ ወመልኦሙ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአብፅዓ ፡ ስባሔሆሙ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ወእግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ ዘአጽንዖሙ ፡ ለአድባራት ፡ ወአኀዜ ፤ ማዕምቃት ፡ በኅቡአታቲሁ ፡ ሰሐቤ ፡ ብርሃኖሙ ፡ ለከዋክብት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወመራሔ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ለአብርሆተ! ፡ መዓልት ፡ ወወርኅኒ ፡ ለአዋክዮተ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወፈ ጻሜ ፤ ፍሬያታት ፡ ዘአግብኦ ፡ ለዮርዳኖስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ወተፅዕነ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አፍላጋተ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ዘአጽንዖ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ዲበ ፤ ኩኩሕ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዛቲ ፤ ኩኩሕ ፡ አሐንፃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያንየ ፡ ወአናቅጸ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ኢይጌይልዋ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ በረከት ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘተካየደ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ተለዓለት ፡ ዛኩ ኩሕ' ፡ እምነ ፡ ፪ አእባን ፡ እለ ፡ አፍአ ፡ እምታቦት ፡ ዘአማሕፀነ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ወመራጥተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ገብአ ፡ እምፄዋ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ዳንኤልኒ ፡ ወደሳ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ርኢክዋ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ተበትከ ፡ እብን ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወኢነከየ ፡ እንታክተ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ዘተበትከ ፡ እምኔሃ ። እግዚእነሂ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወደሳ ፡ ለኩኩሕ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይሰ ምዖ ፡ ለነገርየ ፡ ወየዓቅቦ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጠቢበ ፡ ዘነደቀ ፡ ቤቶ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ውኅዙ ፡ አፍላጋት ፡ ወነፍሑ ፡ ነፋሳት ፡ ወገፍዕዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወኢወ ድቀ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሱሩር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ።
rich, excellent, and beautiful in the midst of the monks who (are) filled with the word (made) of the glorification of God night and day and completely. This took place thus: (there is) the father of the monks and head of the monasteries of Pbow, the pure elder Abba Pachomius (Päkum) for the consecration of whose church we were sent. While we (were) in Alexandria before encountering the grace of this place and before its light shone for us, and while we were sent into the monastery of these monks to receive their blessing, behold it happened that we were sent and we arrived at the community of the angels, guardians of the palace of their Lord. We came from Alexandria because of the (spiritual) labor of the elder monk and his children. We found on our path a solid foundation on a rock. Behold, a wise man has made for us a solid and beautiful construction on the rock in truth. We came from Alexandria because of the cells of the monks similar to the angels by their hymns and their glorifications (addressed) to God. By the grace of God, (we came) by the way of the river until we had reached the memorial of the coming of our Savior by which we were liberated. He gave to the monks the good of abstinence and filled them with the power of the Holy Spirit. He made happy their glorifications to God the Father and to the Lord Jesus Christ who strengthened the mountains, (he) who dominates the abysses by his mysteries, who draws the light of the stars to himself, who guides the sun so that it illuminates the day and the moon so that it illuminates the night and who brings the fruits to term, he who made the Jordan retreat when it saw him, who ascended upon the rivers of Egypt, who strengthened Peter upon the rock. He revealed to him his glory and said to him: "Upon this rock, I will build my Church and the gates of hell will not prevail against it." And again concerning this rock of blessing: it is the one (upon) which he established the covenant with Peter, he came and stayed upon it. This rock is higher than the two stones that (are) outside the altar, in which Jeremiah the prophet deposited the vessels of the altar and the keys until the moment when he would have returned from the captivity of Babylon. Daniel also praised a rock and said: "I, I saw a rock and behold a splinter detached from it without the hand of a man and it did not damage this rock from which it detached." Our Lord Jesus Christ also praised a rock and said: "Whoever hears my word and keeps it resembles the wise man who built his house upon a rock. When the torrents flowed and the winds blew and attacked this house, it did not fall, for it is founded upon a rock."

Chapter 4 (Arabic 91, 169) And you, rock, the waters flow under your foundations and the winds blow upon you, but it has not been changed, your blessing which shines upon us. None of these two things could oppose the One who dwelt upon this rock. On the contrary, like servants, they come prostrate before the right hand of the Creator which (is) imprinted upon this rock like the mark of kings; indeed, when we see the servant of a king and the name of the king written with him - and this one is known -, does not whoever sees him honor him in all things although there is nothing apart from his name and his mark? If this thing happens for an earthly king who is glorified without being seen - (yet) his glory is transitory and vain, this mark of his which (is) his is perishable, behold he is erased from among the kings, he himself (is) thrown into the tomb, (but) his memory remains forever -, how much more therefore (must it be for) the greatness of this action of the Lord for us (which is) the mark of the eternal king of the heavens and of the earth, His right hand which fills the universe (and) that the abysses fear to mention. The One before whom hell humbles itself, for Him the magi burned their books. The name of our Lord Jesus (is) sweet in the mouth of all those who love His name, a name which descended from the very high heavens and (which) the angels announced, a name which made the water Wine by its sweetness, a name that the angels glorify in the heavens, (whose) glory the seraphim proclaim - the principalities, the thrones, the lordships and the powers are prostrate before His power -, name of the Lord Jesus Christ which surpasses all creatures in every place of judgment, sweet and honored name in the mouth of His Father and sweet in the mouth of His mother. This name is completely sweet and (is) a sign that will never be limited. Behold we see, when lightning occurs, that it comes from the sky, and one distinguishes it because behold that manifests from the sky. Likewise concerning the thunder: this one is not hidden on earth, for it manifests in the heights. How much more (does one see) the grace of God which manifested upon the waters: at the moment when they saw it, they prostrated themselves before it and bowed toward the earth. He (= Christ?) came into the deserts and welcomed the monks to Him. Sweet name, when the fragrances of the Lord Christ diffused, the hand of the Lord touched this rock and became a mark of His coming into the land of Egypt, and the blessing of the Lord manifested in this place.
ወኮነት ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአስተርአየት ፡ በረከተ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝክቱ" ፡ መካን ። ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ዘንሕነ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ዮም ፡ ተለዓለት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ እንተ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ፈልፈሉ ፡ ማያት ፡ ወሰትዩ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽምዖሙ ። ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ትመስል ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ እንተ ፡ አፍለሳ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ እመካና ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወኢሕማም ። ዛቲ ፡ ጾላዕ ፡ ትከብር ፡ እምነ ፡ ጾላዕ ፡ ዘአንቅዓት ፡
This rock upon which we (find ourselves) today is very high; it is like the rock from which the waters gushed, and the people of Moses drank from it because of their thirst. This rock resembles the rock that Moses brought out from its location without pain or effort. This rock is honored more than the rock which made flow


[p. 210] {#po.49.p210}
መዓረ ፡ ወሴሰየት ፡፤ ለሕዝብ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ገዳም ። አማን ፡ ኦጾላዕ ፡ ተለዓልኪ ፡ ወተሰባሕኪ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሎን ፡ ገዳማት ። ለመነኮሳትሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳማት ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ኅዱራነ'" ፣ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወይውሕጥዎሙ ፡፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ የኀልፉ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ። አንቲሰ ፡ ተሰባሕኪ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፥፤ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አፍላጋት ። ምስለ ፡ መኑ ፡ ኣስተማስለኪ ፡ ኦጾላዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ታቦርኑ ፡ ዘሐነፁ ፤ ውስቴታ ፡ ሠላሰ ፡ ምጽላላተ ። አሐተ"! ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወካልእተ”" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወሣልስተ"' ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ ኤል ያስ' ፣ ዘኮነ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ዘምንኩስና ፡ ወአንኩርኩረ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዘፍናዌ ፡ ጾላዕ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ አድባረ ፡ ምዕራፎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልኩ ፡ በውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወይዝክርዎ ፡ ለዘቀደሞሙ ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ አርአያ ። ለኤልያስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምዕራፉ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ንእስት ፡ ወንጽሕት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረሐብ ፡ ወጽምዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽገብ ፡ እምተድላ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወከማሁ ፡ ለሙሲሴኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘተውሀቦ ፡ ሕግ ፡ በመልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕኬ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአብርሃ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። ወነሥአ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ብርሃናዌ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ ለደብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ፍሕመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ብሩህ ፤ ወተሰምዩ ፡ ፪እምነ ፡ እብን ፡ ክብርት ፤ ወእልዘይረገድ ፡ ወእልመሃ ፣፤ ወእልክምሰት" ። እልኩቱ” ፤፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ይሰመዩ ፡ አአባነ ፡ ክቡራነ ፡
honey and nourished the people in the desert. In truth, O rock, you are higher and glorified more than all the deserts. As for the monks who were in the deserts, the demons resided in them and devoured all those who passed near them. You, you are glorified more than all the waters. With whom shall I compare you, O rock? Is it with the rock of Tabor in which they built three shelters? One for the name of the Lord Christ, the second for the name of Moses the prophet and the third for the name of Elijah who first donned the monastic garment, rolled a great stone which (is on) the path of the rock and made the mountains the dwelling of the monks so that they might give themselves to adoration and remember the one who had preceded them in this example. The dwelling of Elijah was upon a small and pure rock, with thirst and hunger so that he might be satiated with the delights of eternal life. Likewise, concerning Moses the prophet, it is upon a rock that the law was given to him. The glory of God shone upon him (who was) upon the rock. (This one) took on a luminous aspect and became entirely a mountain like a glowing ember of fire. Two of the stones were called "the honored", 'elzayragad, 'elmaha and 'elkemsat. All these (stones) are called "precious stones", for they received from the light of God which shone upon them.
እስመ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ውእቶሙ ፡ እምብርነሃነ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘአብርሃ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ናሁኬ ፤ እሎን ፡ ሠላስ ፡ ስምዓታት ፡ ደደቃሃ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዘእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ዘሙሴ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ዘአበ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወመኩንኖሙ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፡ ኤልያስ ። እስመ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወኤልያስ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ካህናቲሆሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ ወጸሎታቲሆሙ ፡ ውኩፋት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ ዘግሐት ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወአርኃወታ ፡ ዳግመ ። ጸሎቱኒ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ረሰየታ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ታብቁ ፤ አፉሃ ፡ ወተሐጦሙ ፡ ለዳታን ፡ ወለአቤሮን"” ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አስተማሰሎሙ ፤ መድኃ ኒነ ፡ ለካልእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ካልኡ ፣በከመ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘይጸርሕ"" ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘአፈቅር ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ ስምፅዎ ። ወእልክቱ ፡ ፪ ነቢ ያት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ደመና ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይሴ ብሕ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ዞ እምአርድእአት ፡ አመ ፡ ዓርጉ ፡ 'ዲበ ፡ ደብር” ፣ ምስለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡፤ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዓለም ፡ ብሑትየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይክድኖ ፡ ለማዕምቅ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ 'እምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም"! ፡ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይሰብሑኒ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ቃለ ፡፤ ሕይወት ፡ እንተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ሠርጐ ፡ ከዋክብት ፣፤ ወእምቅድመ ፡ አስተርእዮታ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ። ወከዊኖቱ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ዘይእቲ ፡ መስተናግር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ 'ዘንተ ፡ አርድእት” ፡ ወድቁ ፡'ዲበ ፡ ገጻቲሆሙ” ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተሀውከ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወወጠነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመሰው ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘሣረረ ፡ መሠረታቲሃ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣
Behold therefore these three testimonies occurred for this rock: one (is) that of the Lord Christ, the other, that of Moses, the third, that of the father of the monks and their head, that is to say Elijah. Indeed, Moses and Elijah (are) the heads of the priests (?) of the prophets and their prayers are accepted by God. The prayer of Elijah closed the sky and reopened it. As for the prayer of Moses the prophet, it caused the earth to open its mouth and swallow Dathan (Dätän) and Abiram (‘Abëron). Because of that, our Savior compared them to one another, as (is) witness the Father who cries out in the sky and says: "This is my son whom I love, listen to him." These two prophets heard from the midst of a cloud this testimony from the Father: this one glorified himself alone. Likewise, three of the apostles, when they went up onto the mountain with our Lord, heard the voice of the Father, this one teaching them the glory of the Son by saying: "This is my beloved Son before the universe exists, He is my unique (Son) before the darkness veiled the abysses, this is my Word before all the universe and before the angels praised me, word of life which (is) mine before the adornment of the stars and before the appearance of the universe because of the Word of the Father." The Intercessor (pronounced) for His Son

(His) testimony which follows. When the apostles heard that, they fell on their faces because they had heard the voice of the Father. Then the mountain was shaken and began to dissolve, because it is the One who founded the foundations of the earth who had spoken these words. At the same moment, the stones rolled and cried out because of the word of the Creator. When the Son saw that behold the mountain was shaken and leaping and (that) the apostles had fallen on their faces in fear, He stretched out His hand upon the mountain and strengthened it. (This one) did not begin to totter again, it was firm and enclosed the universe. He turned toward the stones and the rocks, and they were firm so that they did not turn (anymore) hither and thither as if (they were) rams whose creation He had ordered. They too cried out in their turn. Likewise (did) the rocks which (were) in view of this rock upon which the word of the Lord had occurred. They had heard the word of the Lord upon it, they were shaken, began to become fragments and began to dissolve like wax which (is) before the fire. When the only-begotten Son saw that the rocks were shaken, He gave them an order and fixed for them boundaries not to be crossed by (His) wisdom. And again, when He saw that behold the apostles had fallen and were like drunkards because the mountain had (made) them turn like a water wheel - at that moment, they knew that He had strengthened the mountains and the rocks -, He seized Peter and made him rise, He awakened John and, as for James, He reunited him to them. They saw that in which they were by the grace that the Lord had made reside in them. At that moment, they saw His glory and they saw the mountains, the rocks and the glory that the Creator had shown to His apostles so well that they saw these wonders. And again, when people saw children crying on the mountains, they pressed Him (= the Lord) by saying: "Enjoin your apostles to be silent." The Lord said to them: "In truth, I tell you: if these children are silent, the stones will speak." Indeed, it is known that there is nothing impossible between His hands and (those of) His excellent Father and (of) this Holy Spirit
ክፍል፡፳። ወሶበ ፡ ርኢነ ፤ ብዙኀ ፡ እምኩኩሐት ፡ በውስተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ዘክብርት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ የዐቢ ፡ ክብራ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡፣ እንተ ፡ ወግዓ' ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ የማኑ ፡ ሉዓ ላዊ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወናሁ ፡ ሰማዕነ ፡ በእንተ ፤ አቤሜሌክ ፡ ኢትዮጳዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እምገነተ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፍሬያት ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ዘኩሉ ፡ ፅለት ። ወርእየ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ወላዕሌሃ ፡ ጽላሎት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወነበረ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ጽላሎታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኑም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አስፈሬዳ ፡ ዘፍሬያት ፡ ዘእግዚኡ ፡ ወረ ሰየ ፡ መልዕልቴሁ ፡ መክፈልቶ ፡ ወቄጸሮ ፡ በዓፅፉ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፤
to whom (belongs) the glory for ever and ever. Chapter 5 (Arabic 97, 171) Even if we have seen many rocks in Israel which (are) very honored, however greater is the honor of this rock upon which rested His (= Jesus') very high right hand. Behold we have heard concerning Abimelech ('Abëmëlëk) the Ethiopian that he came from the garden of his master (having) with him fruits according to his habit of every day. He saw a rock upon which (there was) shade, he rested there. He settled under its shade to sleep because of the heat, took the basket of fruits of his master, made of

the upper part of its (contents) his share and hid it in his cloak. Indeed, when he came to the garden of his master to take fruits for the house of his master, he took his share, placed (it) on top and said: "This (is) the share of my master. As for my share, I reserve (it) for my father Jeremiah ('Érmeyäs) and I will throw it into the pit of mud." To the poor whom he met, he gave (alms) from his share. What remained, he brought to Jeremiah so that the lord might bless him and that the blessing of Jeremiah the prophet might be great and doubled upon him. When he arrived at this rock - he was coming by the way of the city -, he placed the basket of fruits which (was) for his master under his head and covered it with leaves. What was his own share, he put (it) on the basket of fruits and fell asleep. The grace of the prophet Jeremiah touched him and the helping force which protected the prophet by strengthening him in the pit of mud was with him. It came to Abimelech and (this one) fell asleep near the rock. The fragrance of the fruits of the prophet comforted (him) to the point that the soul of this one was in the perfume. Chapter 6 (Arabic 97, 171) When thirty-five years had passed, he (being) in sleep, since they had taken Jeremiah and the people into captivity to Babylon, Jeremiah looked at the people who (were) there (= in captivity). He remembered the benefits of Abimelech and groaned saying: "Where (are) you, my son Abimelech today? These few fruits that you brought me, how I would like them now so that this people who is afflicted might rejoice in them!" Immediately, this remark arrived at the helping force which was for him (= Abimelech) a protector and he heard with his ears: the helping force was saying to him: "Abimelech, Jeremiah is calling you, for he has thought of your virtue and he knows what you were doing for him." At the same moment, Abimelech rose trembling and heard the voice which was calling him. He looked then at the basket of dates and he found these fresh like their way of being from before, and he glorified God. He said: "Behold now the fruits are more savory than what they were there (= in the basket) before, they have not been changed by the light of the sun and the heat and have not rotted. Behold the fruits of Jeremiah the prophet are more beautiful and radiant than those which are for my master." He said to himself: "Behold my father Jeremiah is calling me, for behold I have heard his voice with my ears. And now, behold until now I have not delayed since the moment [when I fell asleep. My habit every day is to sleep a little until the moment when it is necessary to prepare the meal of my master, I am going to sleep an hour and finish my nap in the shelter of this rock." He fell asleep for the second time, and it was accomplished the prophecy of Jeremiah the prophet that he had said to him: "You will not have hunger nor thirst and the air will nourish you. You will not see the destruction


[p. 216] {#po.49.p216}
ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኢትርኅብ ፡ አንተ ፤ ወኢትጸምዕ ፡ ወአየር ፣ የሐፅነከ ። ወኢትሬኢ ፡ ምዝባሬ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፤ ወኢትሬኢ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ፄዋዌ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ድ ዓመት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ነዊሞ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አፍለስዎ ፡ ለኤርምያስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አድባራተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወገብአት ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክብራቲሃ ።
of Jerusalem and you will not see the sufferings of the captivity." Seventy years were accomplished while he was sleeping, until the moment when they transferred Jeremiah near the mountains of Jerusalem, and Jerusalem returned to its splendor.

Chapter 7 (Arabic 99, 101, 171, 173) This helping force which was the protector of Abimelech ('Afeytamalak) left its place by exalting him (= Jeremiah). When Jeremiah saw this helping force, he thought of Abimelech and (of) the basket of fruits. Jeremiah said to this helping force: "Awaken Abimelech, for behold he is satiated with sleep." At the same moment, this helping force awakened him. It said to him: "Behold your father is calling you." Abimelech awakened immediately trembling. He saw the basket of fruits (which were) according to their way of being (from before) and he found that behold the city was decorated. As for Jeremiah the prophet, he saw him installed on his chariot. Abimelech said: "This occurred in my thought, to my father Jeremiah; does this people who follows him really exist? Would Zedekiah (= Sédëgeyäs) have brought him out of internment?" And while advancing, he saw him (= Jeremiah). Jeremiah said to him: "My son Abimelech, behold you are satiated with sleep. Rise therefore, my son, for it is not lost, the small reward (for) what you did for me and (for) what you accomplished for me in the days of my trial. Know the truthful sign of this city and of these tents: (it has been) for us today seventy years that we (have) been in captivity while you, you were sleeping." Abimelech had knowledge of the word of Jeremiah the prophet and he was seeing the fruits. (Jeremiah) said to him: "See, my son Abimelech, and give me my share of fruits so that you may know the grace of God and His blessing which covered you, and see what you were doing for your master." Abimelech opened the basket of fruits, gave some to Jeremiah the prophet and noted that the grapes (were) according to their way of being (from before). Jeremiah said to him: "Is this for me?" Abimelech said to him: "Yes, my father." Jeremiah said to him: "What you have done is good." Abimelech looked again at these (fruits which were) thus and understood at that moment how many years had passed

Do you not see the praise and the happiness which fell to this rock and returned to it with the help of God. And again, behold you have heard now the praise of this rock from Jeremiah the prophet that he said in the psalm which (is attributed) to him: Happy is he who has taken your little children and smashed them upon the rock. Indeed, the mountain (is) the dwelling of the dead and, in it, one buries the dead. As for the prophet, he knew, he, the honor of the rock; he called it "rock strength of the mountains, rock

strength of those who have fallen asleep in the Lord Christ, strong rock." You see again Samson (Somson) the strong when he was under the rock. When the people were thirsty, he struck the rock with a donkey's jawbone and the rock made water gush and quenched the thirst of the people who were with him. Gideon (Gédëwon) also and Barak (Bäreq) and Jephthah (Yoftähë) they also prayed to the Most High from upon a rock and (this one) split for them - honey, oil and water (from it) gushed - and they rejoiced because of that. See again Joseph (Yosëf) and the Ishmaelites: they were upon a rock and they were in torment because of thirst. At that moment, Joseph prayed to the God of his fathers Abraham (‘Abrehäm), Isaac (Yeshaq) and Jacob (Yä'qob), and (the rock) produced water and they drank from it. Have you seen what happened to the rock upon which Seth (Set) stood according to the mission of Adam (‘Adäm) his father (given) at the moment of his illness? When Adam died, the rock opened and welcomed the body of Adam upon the order of the Lord. All the mysteries of our father Adam, they inscribed (them) upon the rock and this rock was for Adam softer than wax. See therefore all the honors with which (God) has gratified the rocks; only, the honor of this rock is greater, for the Son put His right hand upon it, as a sign of this covenant forever. If there are martyrs and (one) depicts for them an effigy on wood or a wall, there are people who come to it from (their) country to the country (where it is?) and they are blessed by this effigy which is depicted. How much therefore does it surpass, the hand of the Lord Christ who fashioned man with earth and made him rise from the pit of perdition! The hand which strengthened His saints, behold it is depicted and imprinted upon this rock! O greatness of the honor which welcomes the one who comes to this rock, prostrates himself before it and remembers the blessing of God! Behold you see that if there happens to exist a letter from an earthly king and (that) this letter is recognized as being of the writing of the hand of the king, behold you see that everyone kisses it and executes what (is) in it in the way of commandments. How much therefore does it surpass, the king of kings, the Living One who does not die! Totally great (is) the honor of the One who is greater than the dwelling of the kings, as well as the placement of His fingers; indeed, He is the king of heaven and of earth, the One who put His hand upon this rock. It is necessary therefore that all those who (are) in the land of Egypt come and kiss this figure upon which (is) the name of the Lord and His right hand. If therefore there was a fragment of rock which (came) from the earth, when it saw the Lord on the road when He was going His way - he this rock was going with Him -, it glorified Him and understood that it was He who strengthened the mountains and holds them. Behold it is He who now has sat upon this rock with the very high glory of His divinity. The stone glorified Him without a tongue and said with the tongue of its way of being: "My Lord and king has come to me and I do not have a tongue with which to bless Him like men; behold indeed that He has visited me


[p. 220] {#po.49.p220}
እየ ፡ ወንጉሥየ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወአልብየ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ዘአባርኮ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሐወጸኒ ፡ በመለኮቱ ። እድ ፡ ዘኀደረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዘፈወሰቶሙ ፡ ለድውያን ፡ ወወሀበት ፡ ርእየ ፡ ለዕውራን ፡ ወፈወሰት ፤ ሕሙ ማነ ። የማን ፡ዘበረከት ፡ ዝየ ፡ ይእቲኬ ፡ እድ ፡ ሉዓላዊት ፡ እንተ ፡ ታሴስል ፡ አፅባዕተ ፡ ወትጸይሕ ፡ ለምእመናን ፡ ዓሪገ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ይሰ ግዱ ፡ ላቲ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ 'ሰማያውያን ፡ ሥዩማን" ። እድ ፡ ወሀቢተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መካን ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ኅድረታ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ እድ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ፣ መካን ፣ ማኅደረ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ወመሠርያን ፡ ወለጽዋዕያን ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ ምግባራት ፤ እኩያን ። ወሶበሰ ፡ ኅደረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ የማኑ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእይዋ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ እለ ፡ ኅዱራን ፡ ውስተ ፡፤ ደብር ፡፤ ተኃሠሥሠ ፡ ወጠየቁ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ በነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እመዋ ፅለ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት፤! ፡ ኢያስተርአየት ፡ ለነ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት ። ወዛቲሰ ፡ ትእምርት ፡፤ ይእቲኬ ፡ እንተ ፡፤ ትሰድደነ ፡ ለነ ፡ ወመስተቃርንት ፡ ለምግባራቲነ ። ወይእዜኒኬ ፡ አልብነ" ፡ ንብረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እምድኅረዝ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፤ እምትእምርቱ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ። ወበከመ ፡ ተፈሣሕነ ፡
of His divinity." The hand that He placed upon this rock, it is it that healed the sick, gave sight to the blind and healed the suffering. The right hand which blessed here is certainly the very high hand which suppresses wars and clears for the believers the ascent toward the height of the heavens. It is it before which all the celestial chiefs prostrate themselves. The hand which dispenses grace to all men came to this place and became a sign for all the men of Egypt forever. Before this hand dwelt here, this place was the dwelling of demons, of magicians, of invokers (of the devil) and of all evil actions. But when our Lord dwelt in this place and (that) the mark of His right hand was upon this rock, when the demons who dwell in the mountain saw it, they argued, recognized this grace and said to themselves: "Since the days of Solomon son of David (Däwit), this mark has not appeared to us. As for this mark, it is certainly the one which will chase us and will oppose our actions. Now we have (no more) dwelling in this place after that because of what (is) there in the way of a mark of the king of heaven and of earth. Just as we rejoiced at the disappearance of Solomon from the earth, behold now it has happened that the One who opposes us is greater than Solomon."

"I also saw the glory of the Lord; I became like wax which (is) before the fire without it being me who drew to Him my ... like a servant who deserves to be punished by his master. When He saw me with His merciful eye while I was following Him, He stretched out His hand toward me and blessed me by saying: 'I make your coming into this land (be) the memory of my coming.'" || He also came into this land so that there might be a sign and a wonder which have no end. Happy therefore the one who comes to this place and prostrates himself before the right hand of the Creator and Fashioner that He placed here! That (is) the praise of this rock which is worthy of all (praise). Glory to the power of the holy Trinity, the Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit, from now until forever, amen!

Chapter 8 (Arabic 105) After all that, let us return therefore now to the end of the beautiful discourse which (is) for the generation to come so that it may know how our arrival in this place took place and that it may know the grace of the Lord which fell to us there. Theodoret by a sharp justice. Indeed, these were adversaries of my father Dioscorus who was a firm root in the faith: they exiled him and he died in exile because of his struggle for the faith. || He established my poverty, me Timothy his (spiritual) son without merit, in his place in the days of the pure emperor, friend of God, Zeno. It happened that behold came to him the fragrance of the perfume of the memory of the holy abbä Pachomius and (of) his virtues. He ordered my poverty to go to the monastery of the saint so that his church of the desert of Fâou might be consecrated and (that) the memory of the blessed fathers and of the brother monks who resided there might be celebrated. When this news arrived to me, to me who did not deserve it, I rejoiced greatly that I, I was sent to a man who (had been) honored by God from his childhood until the end of his (spiritual) combat and was crowned with all grace and virtue; and I, while (I was) in Alexandria, I took with me Peter (Pétros) the deacon servant of my father Dioscorus, because he knew many more languages than me and knew the languages of the Egyptians also. When we went up by boat, I, Peter the deacon and the brother monks who had come for the consecration of the church of Fâou, many notables of the city of Alexandria and its chiefs went up with us when they learned that the patriarch was leaving for this affair. They came they too to be blessed by the monks of the district of the South. As we, who had been sent, were going up toward Fâou, we arrived at this rock; the boat stopped there without the will of the sailors. Its prow (?) (was) in front of this rock. I said to the monks who (were) with us: "Have we therefore arrived at the shore of Fâou, the place that we were seeking, the dwelling of the great saint abbä Pachomius (‘Abupäkum)?" They answered by saying: "No, no, on the contrary we are now still far from that region." As for the sailors, half of them went down into the river and (the other) half deployed the sail [15 struggled to make the boat leave the bank and navigate again, but they could not move the boat from its location.

Chapter 9 (Arabic 107) While we were thinking about what had occurred, behold an old man descended quickly from the top of the rock. (He had) upon him a garment of hair which (was made) of camel and goat hair. He was shouting and called me by my name three times, but I, I did not understand his language. to come (on board) with us, for his attire resembles (that of) a monk; (he wants) perhaps to go with us to the desert of Fâou to be blessed by the holy abbä Pachomius, or else perhaps God has held us in this place because of him." At that moment, Peter the deacon translated for me the many words that this old man had said and he said to me: "He praises you much." The old man said to me: "My lord patriarch, behold now that nine weeks (have passed) for me (since) was revealed to me your coming here. It is certainly the Lord God who has held your boat in this place with a dead calm for a divine work." I said to him: "Who indicated my name to you?" He answered me: "It is not surprising (that I know it), for your name (is) mentioned in all the churches of Egypt and in all places, and that is certainly not surprising, for you have been called for the church of the holy abbä Pachomius. What is more surprising than that, (is that) behold you are going to set in this place a solid foundation for the Lord Christ and His mother Our Lady the Virgin Mary. Believe me, (my) father Timothy, it is the holy Gabriel the archangel who pulled your boat to the bank here so that the will of his Lord might be accomplished. Come therefore, descend (to land), O man of God, in peace. Our Lord Jesus Christ will be with you during all the days of your life, for your (patriarchal) see is firm in justice and rectitude and a fire burns before you to burn your enemies who do against you evil and turpitude. Indeed, the grace of your father Dioscorus has come to dwell upon you like (upon) Elisha ('Élsä'e), the disciple of Elijah the prophet, who crossed the river Jordan without the water touching him and resurrected the dead from their tomb. Now it is you who have received the incense of your father, just as your fathers (had received it from all the men of the right faith. Do not consider that they called you for the church of the holy abbä Pachomius. Your coming did not occur for that, but your coming (occurred) for Our Lady the pure Virgin Mary; indeed, she is greater than abbä Pachomius. She (is) the mother of the God of justice, our Lord Jesus Christ who, He, is elevated above all that (is) in the world."
ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ይትሌዓል ፡ እምነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ዓለም። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ 'ወረድነ ፡ እምሐመር፡ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ወአኃው ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ወሖርነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይኤምረነ ፡ በአፅባዕቱ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይብለኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘሰሐበት ፡፤ ሐመረከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃድከ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትገብር ፡ ዝየ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ወመሠረተ ፡ ጽኑዓ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወተዝካረ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፡ አብናከ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኅድረትከ ፤ ውስተ ፡፤ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ዘኢየሐልቅ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ሐላዬ ፡ በርእስየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፡ መኑኬ ፡ አነ ፡ ዘኮነት ፡ ሊተ ፡
When Peter the deacon had said these words to me concerning the old man, immediately we descended from the boat, we and the brother monks, and we went to this rock, the old man indicating to us (the path) with his fingers and saying to me: "O my father patriarch, it is the power of God which pulled your boat here without your will. This (is going to occur) now: you are going to make here a memorial and a solid foundation for you, and an eternal memorial for your patriarchal dignity and for your sojourn in this place, (memorial which) will last forever and will not perish." And I Timothy, I was thinking in myself saying: "Who therefore (am) I, I to whom this grace happens? I do not deserve it, [I who undertook (this journey) for the church of the holy abbä Pachomius - much less than my fathers the patriarchs who (preceded me, and I did not labor, I, like them. Indeed, Dioscorus remained many years persecuted

in Alexandria, and this work did not fall to him. These years during which (he was persecuted), I remember (them), and this (work) did not fall to him during his time (of life) and he served like a servant because of his great modesty." As for my father Theophilus (Tä'ofilos) (he was hindered) in his way of being and by the persecutions by which he was persecuted in the land of Egypt, but this story of Our Lady the Virgin Mary and of her arrival at the mountain of Qosqâm was revealed to him, and (nevertheless) he did not establish a foundation for the memory of the Lord. As for (my) father Cyril (Gêrellos) the father of my father Dioscorus, he came into the district of (Upper) Egypt with me in the days of the father abbä Shenoute (Sinodä) the prophet to condemn Nestorius (Nestur) the heretic whom I excommunicated. He (= Cyril) imagined that (Nestorius) would return from his error into which he had entered, but he did not cease to sink into his error. By the will of God, he died according to the death of Herod (Hërodes). This thing (was) evident, for the holy father had cursed him and had said to him: "Just as your tongue has blasphemed Our Lady the Virgin, (likewise,) may your tongue be buried with you in the earth and may your teeth not help you (anymore) during your life." Immediately, his (= Nestorius') mouth was struck, his tongue came out, fell onto his chest from his mouth and was like the breasts of a woman, it gave milk; it produced pus and worms and he died. As for the death of the holy father Dioscorus, the great star, it did not happen in this place.

On the other hand, all that (happened) for me, me the poor Timothy, while I had come to the consecration of the sanctuary of the holy abbä Pachomius, according to what (God) had willed. Who (am) I therefore, me the wretched, for it to happen to me (this) according to what this old monk spoke and according to what we have seen of the station of our boat here? And again, having risen, I prostrated myself before God upon this rock, I prayed to the living God and I asked my fathers the patriarchs that they implore the living God and Our Lady the holy Virgin Mary mother of God for me so that (she) might reveal herself to me in this place and appear to me or, if not (to me), to one of the brother monks, for they (are) purer than me and perfect and they dwell in (their) places with the angels. I asked while speaking thus: "O my Lord Jesus Christ and my king, it is You who established a covenant with Peter Your disciple by saying this: Upon this rock, I will build my Church. Now, if it is Your will, O my Lord, that Your name be blessed upon this rock, may (that) appear therefore and be revealed to me, or if not to the one whom You will have chosen, for to You (are) the power and the glory for ever and ever, amen."

Chapter 10 (Arabic 113) When I had said that, I arrived upon this rock and slept until the moment (of the return) of the light. The Lord God did not push back my prayer, on the contrary He remembered my fathers and the mercy that He had done to them: it was revealed to me the miracle of the descent (of Mary, miracle lasting) until forever. The Lord Christ knower of hidden things knows; it is public, (His) testimony for me. He is my hope and my refuge; it is He who will judge the living and the dead, according to this testimony that I saw upon this rock in a nocturnal vision. It is therefore this: I saw Our Lady the Virgin, behold she was descending from the sky, she (was) crowned with a great light in the assembly of the angels. Then this mountain shone entirely with the light of her chariot, like the light of the sun; she called me by my name by saying: "Timothy, awaken so that I may tell you my glory: I am the mother of God who dwelt, as well as my Son Jesus Christ, upon this rock, in the time of His childhood during our coming into the land of Egypt. Do not be surprised, Timothy, of the fact that I appeared, I, to your father Theophilus, taught him the story of our coming to the mountain of Qosqâm and came to this place. Know this, Timothy, your father and the father of your father will gather with you and with all the prophets to praise me; indeed, my glory (is) in their mouths, my hymn, in the mouth of all the (celestial) hosts, my glory, in the mouth of the cherubim and my eternal joy, in the mouths of the seraphim. I heard (praise) my virtue from the mouth of the virtuous Father when I prostrated myself before Him the day of my death, like all men, when the seven archangels and their hosts sang hymns before me and made glorifications at the walls of paradise. The tree of life prostrated itself before me and carried me on its branches; indeed, my manifestation (is) in the sky and my name forever on earth."
እስመ ፡ አስተርእዮትየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወስምየ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ። አነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወላዲቱ ፡ ለአምላክ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ ወምው ታን ። አነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዘእምኅበ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ብፅዕት ፡ ወአነ ፡ ወለተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘተወ ለድኩ ፡ እምኢያቄም ፡ ዘእምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተወለድኩ ፡ በሩካቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወወሀቡኒ ፡ ብፅዓተ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እምአጥባተ ፡ እምየ ። ወአእመርኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ዘበጽሑፍ ፡ ወድንግልና ፡ እምዘካርያስ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወስምዖን ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ አወፈዩኒ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ፀራቢ ። ወኮንኩ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ እገብር ፡ ግብረ ፡ እዴየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ማኅደርየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መላኬ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትሕትናየ ፡ ለአመቱ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ አሜሃ' ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ለገብርኤል ። ወናሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ይሰምዕ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ዙሉ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወቨ ለወርኃ ፡ መጋቢት ፡ በከመ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወዜነወኒ ፡ ምጽአቶ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ኅቤየ ። ባሕቱ ፡
I am Mary the Virgin, the mother of God, the King of the living and the dead. I am she who was vowed (to God) by her parents, and I am the daughter of a barren woman, I who was begotten by Joachim ('lyägëm) who (was) from Jerusalem, and who was begotten by a carnal union like all men. I was vowed to the Temple from my mother's breast. I learned all knowledge that (is recorded) in writing and (the respect for) the virginity of Zechariah (Zakäreyäs) the priest and of Simeon (Sem'on) until the day I was given to Joseph (Yosëf) the pure old man, the carpenter. In his house, I did my work in my dwelling. When He who rules over all saw my humility, (to me) his servant who am Mary the mother of the King of kings, then he sent Gabriel to me. Behold, he hears my voice here, for he (is) all justice: he came to me on the twenty-ninth of the month of maggäbit, as it is (written) in the Holy Scriptures, and announced to me the coming of the King to me. But I do not know how the work of the Lord took place in me, and he did not tell me how he would come to me nor at what time, but he came

to me and dwelt in me; I remained a long time with this matter. In the end, the matter was revealed to me when I went to Elizabeth ('Élsäbet). I was frightened and astonished at the word of Gabriel, (at) his greatness and (at) his majesty. When my Lord came to me - he (was) on the chariot of the cherubim -, I did not know at all the height of his work, and his power which he had placed in me was not considered by my mind, for I did not know the needs of women nor what they desire at the time of their pregnancy. I never saw in my breast the sign of conception as women (have it), my belly did not grow, and I never took off the garment that was on my body. He came to me, and I do not know how he came and dwelt in my belly. I did not hear his voice, I did not see his chariot, I did not see his throne, I did not know that he had dwelt in my belly until my kinswoman Elizabeth made known to me what it was about him at the moment I went to her, according to the word of Gabriel the angel (addressed) to me. When she saw me, immediately she cried out with a loud voice and said, "Blessed (are you) among women and blessed (is) the fruit of your womb. Whence (does) this (happen) to me that you come to me, mother of my Lord?" From that moment, my heart was strengthened and I counted nine months, the time of his (gestation), like all women. When I had returned from her, I did not know how his arrival (would be) until my pearl shone by his will and it enlightened everything: I saw the brightness of his light and I heard his voice. I did not know how I gave birth to him; the mystery of my conception and (that of) my childbirth (are) one thing, and I did not know how either this or that came about.
ወኢዝክቱኒ ፡ ክፍል ፡ ፲ወ8። ወውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘአብጽሐ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ለመወልዲት ፡ አብርሃ ፡ ቤዘ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ ወሰብሑ ፡ ልደቶ ፡ ዘእምኔየ ፡ ወአብኡ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ 'ወከርቤ ፡ ወዕጣነ' ፡ እሉ ፡ ዘአብኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለወልድየ ። ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ስሒን ፡ ዘቀርበ ፤ ለወልድየ ፡ ወካልኡ ፡ ሞጻሕት ፡ ዘዓርገ ፡፤ ለወልድየ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ። ወኮነ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ፉርባን ፡ ዘዓርገ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወሰረየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ኃጣውኢሆጮ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሞጻሕት ፡ ዳግሚት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ ዘዓርገት ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደመ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ ዘቀተሎሙ ፡ ሄሮድስ ። ዝኩ ፡ ዘአንሥአ ፡ ድን ጋፄ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወቀተለ ፡ ደቂቆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሰገል ፡ አመርዎ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ትምይንቶ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ወረሰይዎ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ይትዓቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያእምር ፡ 8ኒ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡
Chapter 11 (Arabic 115, 117) The angel who had brought me the midwife made a star shine in the east, the magi came to him (= Jesus), glorified his birth which (came) from me, and offered him gold, myrrh, and incense; these are the (products) that they offered for the birth of my Son. The first (gift is) the incense that (the magus) offered to my Son, the second (is) the libation that was presented to my Son, the day of his birth, by the magi. It was the first offering that was presented to the Father the day of the birth of my Son by the magi. It came to pass and (God) forgave them their sins. And again, the second libation that came to pass for my Son, which was presented to the Father, is the blood of the little children that Herod killed. That is what stirred up a great trembling in all Israel. || killed his (= Israel's) little children because of my Son Jesus; indeed, the magi indicated to Joseph his ruse when they came, prostrated themselves before him (= Jesus), and made Joseph take heed


[p. 232] {#po.49.p232}
ዘንተ ፡ ምስጢረ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ነጽር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወርኢ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ወሐሊ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወተዓቀብ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ይልእክ ፡ ብከ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃው ፡ አው ፡ ይትኃሠሠከ ፡ ትሑር ፡ ኅቤሁ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የሐይጠከ ፡ ወይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ ለሕፃን ። እምቅድመ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ደደቀ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ግብር ። ወዮሴፍ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተሰደድነ ፡ እምሄሮድስ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ።
that no man should know this mystery. They said to him: "Look well, see this little child and take good care of what concerns him; take heed if Herod sends you (a message) with the brothers (7) or if he asks you to go to him. Indeed, he will deceive you and will want to kill the little child. Before this, a great torment happened because of this matter." Joseph the old man told me this word when we were persecuted by Herod because of him (= Jesus).
እብለከ ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኀዘናት ፡ ወሕማማት ፡ እለ ፡ ተመንደብኩ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ኢረከብዎሙ ፡ ለአራዊት ፡ ጥቀ ። ወበኩሉ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አነ ፡ ውልው ልት ፡ ወእፈልስ ፡ እመካን ፡ ኅበ ፤ መካን ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወእነሥኦ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ ምስ ሌሁ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አግዋርየ ፡ ወአዝማድየ' ፡ ፈሪህየ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ እምሄሮድስ ፡ ወእምሐራሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ እሰምዕ ፡ አውያቶን ፡ ለእማተ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ ወእማንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተማርራ ፡ ወያስቆቅዋ ፡ እሬእያ' ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታንቀለቅል ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ወእፈ ርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጽኡ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወትሬእየኒ ፡ ፍልሰት ፡ እምቤት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤት ፡ አነ ፡ ወወልድየ ፡ ወፍቁርየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ። ወአሐውር ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እርከቦን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ ወሕፃናቲሆን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ወልድ ፣፤ ወእማንቱ ፡ ያስተማርራ ፡ ሞቶሙ ፡ እኪተ ። ወአሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፤ ገሊላውያን ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ ወእረክቦን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልባሲሆን ፣ ሥጡጣት ፡ ወአርእስቲሆን ፡ ዕሩቃት ፡ ወእማንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይላህዋ ፡ ወያስተማርራሆሙ ፡ ለሕፃናቲሆን ። ወአሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔ ምኒ ፡ ወእረክቦን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ ወናሁ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አውያቶን ፡ ኅበ ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወእማንቱ ፡ ይረግማሁ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡ አርዌ ፡ እኩይ ። ወአሐውቶር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢይረክባ ፡ ለእኅትየ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥኒ ፡ ተከብተት ፡ እምነ ፡ ፍርሃቱ ፤ ለሄሮድስ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ኑፉቅ ። ወኩሉ ፡፤ መካን ፡ ዘአሐውር ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እምአድያሚሃ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እረክብ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ ውሉዶሙ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፡ ተቀትሉ። ለአሐቲ ፡እረክባ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ወትራፈ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ምስለ ፡ ሐራ ። ወለካልእትኒ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እደ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቀተ ልዎ ፡ አኀዘቶ ፡ ወላዲት" ፡ ባቲ ፡ ፳ ወልድ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወቀተልዎ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡እንዘ ፡ ትሬእዮ ። ወለካልእትኒ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ እጓለ ፡ ማውታ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አብ ። ወካልእትኒ ፡ ትብል ፤ እስመ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ አጥባትየ ፡ አስርሐኒ ፡ ወየሐምመ፻" ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ ። ወፍጹመ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ሕማማት ፡ ወኀዘናት ፡ ዘገብሮሙ ፡ ሄሮ ድስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅትለተ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አምላክ ፡ አስተርከበ ፣፤ ፍትወተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ እስራኤላውያን ፣ ወኢኃሠሥዎ ፡
I tell you (this), Timothy, the pains and the sufferings with which I was tested with my Son, they did not touch even the wild animals. Every day, (I was) agitated and I went from one place to (another) place with him; I took him and I went with him to the house of my neighbors and my relatives, fearing Herod and his soldiers for him. When I heard the cries of the mothers of the little children - they were exasperated with pain and were lamenting -, I saw my soul wavering far from me and I feared that they would come to me also, and the flight saw me passing from one house to (another) house, me and my beloved Son Jesus. I went to Jerusalem to find women and their breasts (7) without a child - they were exasperated with pain over their dead male (child). I went to the Galileans, for they (are) sons of my people, and I found the women, their clothes torn and their heads bare - they were lamenting and exasperated with pain over their little children. I went to Bethlehem also and I found the women, behold their cries reached up to the gates of heaven: they were cursing Herod, the evil serpent. I went into the land of Judah and did not find my sister Elizabeth, for Elizabeth also had hidden herself for fear of Herod, the hypocritical king. In all the places where I went in the surroundings of Jerusalem, I found affliction for their (= the inhabitants') small children who had been killed. I found one (woman), the head of her child in her hands and the rest of his body with the soldiers. Another woman (had) with her the hand of her son; indeed, when they had killed him, his mother had seized him by it (= the hand), (him her) only son, but they had taken him from her and had killed him while she was watching. The son of another (was) an orphan, without a father. Another still was saying: "The pain of my breasts makes me suffer and the milk that (is) in them torments me." And truly, as for the sufferings and the pains that Herod inflicted for the murder of the little children, there is nothing that is like them. But the Lord God instilled affection in the heart of the Israelites: they did not seek my Son and did not take him from me to give him in place of their children.
ለወልድየ ፡ ወኢነሥአዎ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ የሀብዎ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ደቂቆሙ ። ክፍል፡፲ወ፪። ወእንዘ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአረጋዊ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ፀራቢ ፡ ይትለአከነ ፡ ወይሴስ የነ ። ናሁ፤ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተንሥእ ፡
Chapter 12 (Arabic 119) While we (were) in Judea, the old man Joseph the carpenter served us and fed us. Behold, Gabriel the angel appeared to him saying:

"Rise, take the little child and his mother and go into the land of Egypt, for they are seeking the little child to destroy him." Joseph informed me of what Gabriel had said to him, we rose and took the road that (goes) toward Egypt with much fatigue - we were together and Salome (was) with us. We passed from one place to (another) place among the cities of the children of Israel and their provinces, none of Herod's soldiers met us and no fear of him nor suffering (caused by) him reached us. When we had seen that all Israel was without a male child, we came up to Fastarun which is Bastâ (Bastä), and we were not received (there) at all. The heart of Joseph the carpenter suffered then because of the fatigue of the road and I wept for him saying: "O my beloved Son, behold we are persecuted with all this affliction because of you. What have you done, you, in the way of sin so that, as for this old man who feeds us, his heart grows weak because of the reproach with which they cover him because of us and also because of my weeping, for he has not found rest a single day? Sometimes, I weep because of my beloved Son, because of the fear of Herod's soldiers, sometimes I tremble because of the brigands who follow us. Assuredly, the sorrows with which I am tormented, (no) other mother has been tormented (like) me." When I saw that Joseph began to quarrel with me saying: "Because of you, I have carried beyond my strength what I cannot (carry) in my old age", I, I bent my face downward in the direction of my beloved Son - he was suckling my breasts -, weeping and saying: "O my beloved Son, what is this sin that you have committed, O my beloved Son, so that all this comes upon us because of you, to the point that this old man who watches over us, his heart suffers?" I wept again saying: "What is, I pray you, the sin that you have committed, for behold the heart of this old man suffers?" And as he (= Jesus) was speaking with me, he stopped drinking the milk of my breasts and said: "O my beloved mother, strengthen your heart and do not be afflicted, for all this that happens to you (happens) because of me. But let your heart be strengthened and be like a rock, O my beloved mother, for the humiliation to accomplish which I came into (this) world, I will not leave (it) without having accomplished it. Carry me, O my father Joseph, a little so that I may put my hand on your breast so that your soul may be strengthened." And I, I laughed and said to the old man Joseph: "O my father, do not be angry with me and do not let me carry this little child alone: behold now I am tired, O my father, see how he looks toward you so that you may carry him." When I had said this to the old man Joseph, his anger vanished immediately and he carried the little child, my beloved Son, on his shoulders. At that moment, my Son placed his hand on his (= Joseph's) breast. At that moment, the old man Joseph seized the hand of the little child to kiss it. Immediately, the discouragement withdrew from him, he forgot the fatigue of the road and kissed the

እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በአማን ፡ ኦማርያም ፡ ናሁ ፡ ረከብኪ ፡ ጸጋ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፣ መክብበ ፡ ትዝምድነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለነ ፡ ነዋ ፡ ደለወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናንሶሱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በአማን ፡ ደለወነ ፡ ርእይ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ጸዊሮቱ ፡ ኀበ” ፡ እደውየ ። እስመ ፡ ድካማት ፡ እለ ፡ ተወከፍናሆሙ ፡ ምስሌኪ ፡ ኢይከውን” ፣ መጠነ ፡ ሜጣ ፡ ለስብሐት ፡ እንተ ፡ ሀለዋ ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ ለነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ለነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ናዘዘኒ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ረከበተኒ ፤ ወገነይኩ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ወለአቡሁ ፡ ጌር ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
little child from head to foot. He was kissing him saying: "In truth, O Mary, behold you have obtained more grace than the whole of our race; indeed, behold we have deserved to walk with him in whom there is no sin; in truth, we have deserved to see this one and, more still, that I carry him in my arms. Assuredly, the fatigues that we have experienced with you are not at the height of the price of the glory that is to be manifested to us through him, just as we have seen the glory that was manifested to us through him in Bethlehem." When the old man Joseph said this word, he consoled me. Because of that, I knew that the grace of my Son Jesus had touched me and I adored him as well as his excellent Father and the Holy Spirit, forever.

Chapter 13 (Arabic 121) When we had crossed and passed all these trials that (we had encountered) on this road, we left the land of Israel and came into the land of Egypt, to a place where (there were) thistle thorns. We found (there) a virtuous man, a shepherd, by the name of Joseph. He entertained us at his house for four days, he and his two children fed us. This action was pleasing to the thought of my beloved Son so that he put (his) blessing in this place (covered) with thistle. He planted three trees there and they protected us from the heat on that day. He blessed this place, put his name in this place (covered) with thistle and this place was called Baysus from the name of my Son who dwelt there. With great difficulty, we passed beyond this place thanks to the friendship of this shepherd who was there. From there we continued by going up (toward the south) by this road and reached the road of the river while being in fear of the brigands. We reached a city in which (there were) many idolaters; it was called Bardonâ. We found there an old man who (was) sitting at the gate of this city. This man mocked us with great laughter and he insulted Joseph the old man. When we saw the trials that the people of the city (inflicted) on us and their way of acting with us, we did not eat nor drink the bread and the water that (were) theirs and we did not stay with any of them. There was to the east of Bardonä a stone and it gave (words) of beatification to my Son; it began to roll behind us like someone who advances and it was moving (?) behind us. When my Son saw it moving behind us, he pointed his finger at it and said a word of blessing for it. My Son Jesus pointed again (his finger) toward it and said to it: "I say to you, stone, who do not speak, that never (will you know) fatigue again; rest here until the punishment of my Father comes and he removes all the blows (given) to Egypt."

Chapter 14 (Arabic 123) After that, we passed into another city and fear did not let us dwell there because of the fear that we had of the brigands on the way. We sought the river and arrived in the evening at the bank of the river that (is) to the west; indeed, we had been told that there was an idol temple on our way, that is to say to the west of the river. It was many (sacred) trees and idols. We were afraid to go by their road to Ashmunein (Esmonën) (by taking) this road; indeed, we had to pass near them. We asked the old man Joseph to make us cross to the eastern bank (to see) if we would find a road by which to go up to the city of Ashmunein; indeed, we were afraid to go by this road on which (there were) idols. At that moment, a storm wind rose on the river, there were great waves and the tumult was very great to the point that the waves were colliding with one another. And behold, we saw a small boat in the middle of the river, two brothers were busy with their work in it. Joseph called them saying: "Brothers, have pity on us and (may we) sail with you to the eastern bank, for it is the time of evening." Joseph remained a long time speaking with them, but they did not pay attention to his words. When he had spoken to them for a long time, they answered him with great anxiety saying: "What do you want, innocent old man, do you not see the peril of the waves in which we (find ourselves) and this trial where we (are)?" When my Son saw that they would not take us with them, he stretched out his hand; immediately, the wind fell and the river began to ebb before him. The rock came to us and we crossed the river on it without fatigue. When Joseph saw the grace that had touched us and how we had crossed the river without a boat, large or small, he said to me: "Had I not told you that we have found grace more than the whole of our race? Or do you not see this rock that carried us and how, thanks to a movement of his hand, (the child) made the wind cease before us and before the entire river - the waves ebbing by the sole movement of his fingers? If this one does this action during his childhood, what will therefore happen through him in (his) adult age and when he will reign over Israel - it is he who will judge the living and the dead?" I said to him: "Yes, behold the grace of God is multiplied, for the maker of this wonder, it is I who gave birth to him." When we remained in this place, the light shone: (now) at the time of evening our rest took place on this rock.

Chapter 15 (Arabic 125) And again, we saw a woman magician and those demons who (are) installed there (= on the rock) by stopping the boats and the wind and by taking money from them (= from the navigators) before letting them (continue to) travel. This (woman) was called Daludar (Täludär) and her daughter-in-law, Tamar (Tämär). She dwelt in an idol temple in view of the west (bank) of the river. Behold, she had agitated all men by her magic like Jannes (lyänës) and Jambres (Yemräs) the magicians of Egypt. When she looked at this rock, this magician and her troop of demons who served her - she was tormenting and oppressing whoever went on the river, and from those who were going up (it) and going down (it) she was drawing a tribute thanks to her magic -, and when my Son passed to sit on this rock, when her (= Daludar's) demons saw us, they took fright. Half of them came to Daludar and to Tamar her daughter. They let her know what had happened and what they had seen. She said to them: "Why then (are) you frightened?" They said to her: "Our fear and our dread (are) greater than (they were during) all the days that we (have been) in your service and (in the service) of your daughter-in-law." She said to them: "Tell me what made you afraid." The demons said to her: "This miracle that we have seen today on this rock, we had never seen; this miracle (is) that by which we will be driven out. Since Solomon the king, we have not seen - and it has not appeared - such a thing on the surface of the earth. We rejoiced when Solomon was taken from among us, for he was tormenting us. But what has appeared today is greater than him." The magician said to them: "What (is) this miracle and what does it look like?" The demons said to her: "(It has) the appearance of the palm of a man's hand [ (which) left an imprint after having withdrawn. We do not know if it was in heaven; its appearance (is) the appearance of the hand of Michael our enemy; because of that therefore, it has prevailed over us and, with it, (there is) an image inscribed in the name of the great God. This is what we have seen and behold we have come and informed you (of it)." When the magician heard this, she meditated in her heart and rebuked these demons. Then she called other demons whose total number was sixteen and said to them: "Hasten toward this rock, cut it, detach it (from the stone) all around and plunge it into the depths (of the river). And whoever you find on this rock, bring him to me in chains." When these demons that the magician had sent came to us, they wanted to seize us; my Son Jesus rebuked them and they turned back fleeing sheepishly with great shame. When they returned to her (= the magician) (well) caught and sheepish, they said to her:

"How (can) you (remain) sitting! Behold we have found a little child suckling the breasts of his mother, he is the one who will abolish all the magic that (is) in the world. We have never seen such an example among all the children of women and we have never seen actions like this; indeed, around him (he has no magical equipment) like all the sorcerers: by a single movement of the fingers of this one, we were reduced to nothing and rendered without strength by his gesture." The great magician said, for her part, to her servants the demons: "Woe to you, so this little child has prevailed over you, has conquered you and has driven you out! As for me, now, I will no longer sit until I have seized this little child, his mother and him who is with him, and I will do them violence. Only, hasten to prepare for me a chariot and bring me all (my) servants so that I may go to see this miracle, which (happened) on this rock, which has put you in this trembling." She also said to her priests who were standing before her: "Prepare the temple well for me and light the fire in it for when I return, and I will burn this woman and her son." Immediately, she mounted on this satanic chariot - this one was full of evil spirits - and came to us; with her were many demons, they were in a circle around her and were chanting for her. When she arrived in the middle of the river, my Son made a sign with his little finger that he had stretched out and said: "To you, I say, evil satanic cloud, be destroyed now and dissolved, become like the smoke of the fire and may all that (is) on you go into the depths of hell forever." Immediately, this chariot was destroyed and fell into pieces. As for the magician and her daughter, they descended into the depths of Tartarus forever and the water covered them. Likewise, all her demons went with her into the depths and their memory was erased from off this rock forever.

At that moment, my beloved Son gave me an order, to me Mary, saying: "O my mother, look downward and consider what has happened." When I heard this word from him, I observed and saw: behold all men (were) with head bowed and knees bent before my Son, he (was) in the midst of them. I looked at my arms and said to myself: "Perhaps he has gone with them." I found him suckling my breasts, I then inclined my face toward him to kiss him and found his head between my hands. I raised my eyes to heaven, glorified him (= Jesus) and saw him sitting, all the heavenly chiefs glorifying him; he (was) sitting on his throne. Then I was troubled, I trembled and said: "When did this one arrive to his Father in the heavens?" I hastened to touch him and found his two hands gripping my breasts and him drinking my milk. I inclined my face and kissed him, his head (being) between my breasts. He said to me: "O my mother, know that I (am) in the depths of the earth while (being) in the heights of the heavens. Just as you see me, my hands


[p. 244] {#po.49.p244}
ተኪ ፡ ከማሁኬ ፡ በእዴየ ፡ እኅዝት ፡ አድያሚሃ ፡ ለምድር ። ወይእቲ ፡ እዴየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዘአጽንዓት ፡ ሰማያተ ። ወኢኮንኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ፍሉጠ ፡፤ እምአብ ፡ ወኢአሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ 'ወልድየ ፡ ዘንተ” ፡ ቃለ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ለማዕምቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትራከብ ፤ ካልእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ጸዋልዕትኒ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ዘአእቤለከ ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ኮነ ፡ ለነ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ።
gripping your breasts, likewise my hands grip the ends of the earth. It is this hand of mine also that has strengthened the heavens; I am not separated from the Father for a single hour." When my Son had said this word to me, he ordered the abysses to join one another thus, and likewise the rocks. That which I told you, Timothy, happened to us on this rock.
