Chapter 16 (Arabic 129) After that came to us Rufus ('Ahrufäs), the angel who guards the land of Egypt. He prostrated himself before my beloved Son like someone afflicted, his hands (were) on his head and he was weeping. My Son said to him: "What is the matter with you, Rufus, powerful angel, and why are you afflicted like this while I for my part, I know (the cause of) your pain and (of) your affliction?" Rufus the angel answered saying: "My Lord and my God, I am very weary, I have become miserable more than all the angels; you have instituted me in this country worse than the entire universe; behold the totality of my fields and my vineyards has given thorns and weeds, the waters also have become bitter because of the sin of those who dwell on (this) land. The people of the country have been defiled by sins and the stench of their perfumes has risen above my head, my sun gives darkness because of the perfumes of incense that they offer to idols. The fathers slaughter their children and they pour their blood as a libation to the demons. The brothers fornicate with their sisters. Behold the people of this land have completely accomplished all sins." This is what Rufus said to my beloved Son while accusing the people of the territories of Egypt. He said (again): "O my Lord, I do not desire to remain a single hour (more)!" My Son consoled him and said to him: "Everything you have said (is) true, Rufus, and I, I know them (= the Egyptians) better than you, but be strong for behold your affliction and your pain are removed today. It is not you who will carry their fatigues: henceforth, they will be filled with joy and blessing. If I have come to you today, Rufus, (it is) to purify the entire land of Egypt. I, if I have come, (it is) to cut the root of the idolaters today and send all the idols into the depths, to tear from this land the thorns and the weeds and to burn them by the fire of my divinity. Behold I will make my garden and my vineyards flourish and bear fruit, I will put the incense to (my) Father in this land in place of what was there previously in the way of tears that were numerous there. Behold I will make joy and gladness multiply there in place of what was there in the way of the power of Satan. Behold I will make the angels dwell and be in good understanding with all men thanks to the abundance of the perfumes of the incense that will come from the part of men

pure. Just as Moses my servant, behold I will gratify my servants with a stay in the Garden of a thousand years, they being magnificent thanks to the fragrances of their perfumes. Now, Rufus the angel, rejoice and be in gladness, for behold he has come who will free you from your captivity, will take care of your people and will deliver them from the evil one who has chained them. Behold this country will be called 'land of hope' forever. Now, Rufus the powerful angel, rest from your affliction.” When Rufus the angel heard this from my Son, he rejoiced greatly and cried out with a loud voice saying: "Behold all countries are filled with the glory of God!" My Son ordered the angel to go before us, a staff in his hand, in his right. As for the idol temple of this magician that my Son had sent into the depths, (he) ordered concerning it that it be burned; it was destroyed from top (to bottom) and there remained no trace (of it)

Chapter 17 (Arabic 131) And again, we remained on this rock three days and I poured purifying water there. After three days, toward evening, behold a boat came from the city of Alexandria to this place. When the sailors saw Joseph the old man as well as Salome, they cried out saying: "We conjure you, servants of the great magician, do not stop our boat, for behold brigands have taken everything that was with us before our arrival in this place." If they said this, (it is) by imagining that they (= Joseph and Salome) were among the guards of Daludar, the great magician. The old man Joseph said to them: "Do not be afraid, for behold God has removed the power of this magician woman forever. Even if she had power, behold she has perished and vanished; let your boat (continue to) travel." After Joseph the old man had spoken to them, he boarded their boat (and passed) to the west (of the river). Behold Joseph had attached a pillar to this boat in which we had crossed to the other bank. The rock began to go with us, my Son put his hand on it and said to it: "Stay here and do not move from your place. May this sign not disappear from this place forever, for it is my hand." I did not cease to walk with my Son, Joseph the old man and Salome; we (were) trembling and agitated until we reached the district of Ashmunein, my Son performing wonders and raising the dead. We experienced in the city of Ashmunein dread and affliction: impure bronze horses mocked us and wild animals eaters


[p. 248] {#po.49.p248}
እሙንቱ ፡ አግማል ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ኀቤነ ። ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ እስከ ፡ 'ዕለት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኦሰብእ ፡ በከመ ፡ይቤ ፡ዮሴፍ ፡ አረጋዊ ።
of men which were camels came out toward us. My beloved Son rebuked them and spirits came out of them; they became a sign until this day, O men, as (the) old man Joseph says

And again, Joseph the old man had knowledge of a carpenter during our coming into the land of Egypt, this one welcomed us into his dwelling; there was at his home, in his city, a great gathering of sick people. They were brought to the house of this old man, we took them with us (and took them) to a place that is called the mountain of Qosqâm. (My Son) washed them before (?) this column and they were all healed by the power of my Son. As for me, I went to this location of the column as well as my Son so that we might see the power of the column; immediately, it inclined its head and prostrated itself before my Son. The order was expressed by my Son that his name be in this place as it (is) on the rock until now. The name of this carpenter who had entertained us in his house was Dioneus (Dayänus), he admired the grace that had touched this column. These (events) happened one with the other: the year I was born, the brightness of a star illuminated this place and (the appearance of this star) was (also) the commemoration of our coming to Egypt. I have not found a place where to reveal the birth of my beloved Son apart from this place which is Qosqâm.

After our exit from this place, a great fright happened to us: brigands fell upon us and frightened us greatly by their appearance like (that) of the Berbers. Their appearance was very terrifying, the smallest of them was worse than the big one. When I saw them, I imagined that they (were among) the soldiers of Herod, for their appearance and their swords (were) terrible. The place where they had met us was a desolate and desert place. One of the brigands took my Son and the garment that was on him all at once. The biggest one shouted at him and said to him: "Leave them today and tomorrow, what will come to me as a share, take (it) for yourself." By the will of God, they did not do us harm. The other brigand was standing with my Son and was going away, but his companion... I wept then much saying: "O my father Joseph, all the sorrows with which we were afflicted, behold I have forgotten them." And, at that moment, I remembered the Israelites who were weeping because of their children. I wept again saying: "Woe to me, to my beloved Son, they have taken you from off the shoulders of your mother! Behold my belly has cried and has torn itself, I have no one to help me, O my beloved, I do not know the language of these barbarian brigands and I (am) afraid because of them; I am a virgin woman and they (are) barbarians. I did not know that this death was hidden from you. I, in this desert, I (am) alone; I have never dwelt in a city nor a region - may there be someone who takes me under protection for you! I have no one who

አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ለእኪት" ። ወኩሎን ፡ ግብራቲሁ ፡ ክሥሠታት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወሕሱም ፡ በከመ ፡ ነገረ ተነ ፡ ሰሎሜ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢሰሰለ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ' ሕማምየ ፡ ወብካይየ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘር ኢኩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐላዬት ፡ አነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢኮነት ፡ ይእቲ"'' ፡ ልማ ድየ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ፈያት ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ዘመሠጦ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ በዓመፃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርሕቁ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ሕቀ ፡ ባረኮ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ለፈያታዊ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ እምፅፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወዘከረ ፡ ብካይየ ፡ ወረሰዮ” ፣ ዓርኮሙ ፡ ለኪሩቤል ። ወዘከረ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ወመናዝዘ ፡ እንተ ፤ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ መና ፡ ለልሳኑ ። ወዘከረ ፡ ንግደታ ፡ ለወላዲቱ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ። ወዘከረ ፡ ዕራቆ ፡ እንተ ፡ ገብራ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ ፈያ ታዊ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ከደና ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ በምእልፊተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ፍጹም ። ኦለብዝኃ ፡ ቲሩት ፡ ዘአሰፈወ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ 'ለዝክቱ” ፣ ፈያታዊ ፡ ዘገብሮ ፡ ለነ ፡ እምቲኒሩት ፡ ሕዳጥ።
knows me in this place so that he might know these brigands, my heart and my soul suffer. The Israelite women whose children were killed were older than me; I, I (am) an orphan since my childhood and I have no one with whom to take refuge. I have become like the wild beasts that weep over their little ones when they have been killed, and I have acted like them; but these brigands did not have pity on my weeping. The birds lament when their fledglings have been taken from under their wings; likewise, I also have stretched my hands over my head, but these brigands did not have pity on me and did not have pity on the cries of the wild beasts. [Their (= the beasts') astonishment at their (= the bird killers'?) subject, I (experienced it) like them. These brigands did not have pity on me and I followed them from one place to (another) place." When I saw the nakedness of my Son, I remembered my wandering condition, my weakness and my virginity and I turned backward. Joseph the old man was looking at what had descended upon me of affliction and suffering, he was lowering his head downward, hiding his face with his cloak and weeping. As for Salome, she took her cloak that had fallen from off her and hid her face with it, for she was bareheaded. The Lord God put pity in the heart of the biggest brigand and (this one) went toward the smallest and quarreled with him. He took my Son and his clothes and brought them to me. He carried my Son on his arms and kissed him, he pronounced with us many words of consolation, and also with Joseph the old man. As for him who had taken my Son, he was a wicked brigand. His eyes resembled the color of glass and his eyes inclined (?) toward evil. All his acts revealed that he was wicked and evil, as Salome had told us. But my suffering and my tears did not withdraw from me from having seen the action of this (man); indeed, I (was) preoccupied with the fact that it was not my habit to look at any man, much more a brigand and much more which had taken my Son from me by violence. When they had moved a little away from us, my Son blessed the big brigand and gave him a share of the tree of life. He remembered my tears and made him the equal of the cherubim. He remembered the sweet and consoling words that he had said to me and gave him manna for his tongue. He remembered the wandering of his mother and gave him (= to the brigand) a place of rest in paradise. He remembered his (= his own) nakedness that the wicked brigand had provoked against him when he had taken his clothes (and) he covered himself with a myriad of perfect lights. O (glory) to the abundance of benefits that he promised to this brigand (for) what he accomplished for us in the way of a small benefit!

ወውእቱ ፡ ድፉን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወኢነበርነ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝኩ!" ፡ መካን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ሕዳጥ ። ወናሁ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለገብር ኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኅቤነ ፡ ወአመረተነ ፡ ሐጉጐሎ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡ ወገባእነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም' ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ ፍቁር ።
Chapter 18 (Arabic 137) When we had advanced a little after the trial with which we had been tormented by the little brigand and (after) the benefit that the big brigand had accomplished for us, my Son knew the thought of my heart. He said to me: "My mother, may your heart not be sick because of what happened to us from the part of the brigand, for this (is) the will of my Father and I will not abandon (it) before (having accomplished it). Of these two brigands one (is) satanic, for he is of the seed of the heretics. Now, my mother, may your heart not be afflicted because of this humiliation that I have received, for this one is an order from the Father. Be enduring with me, in my tribulations in this world, for you (are) the gate of Jerusalem, you are the consoler and the luminous garment that does not perish." My Son said (thus) to me many words of consolation while we (were) on the road to the mountain of Qosqâm. When we reached it, we began to walk here and there, hoping to find a good place to dwell there. My beloved Son pointed with his fingers to a place and said: "Let us go toward this good place to dwell there, for it is a very good place, my mother. This one is (in) the tribe of Judah, the root of the family from which I am issued; it is worthy of my blessing more than all the (other) places that (are) ours." When we were settled there, I found there a small corner in the upper part, (it) had an opening on its north side. I found there everything that women need to wash their children: a basin, a pitcher, a tub and a well of water. When it was the ninth day since we had descended to the mountain of Qosqâm, Dioneus the carpenter friend of Joseph the old man came to us perched on his mount; he had with him a little of what we needed. We told him about what the brigands had done to us and their appearance; he knew them and he said: "These two brigands dwell on the side of Ashmunein, they are brothers one to the other as (being) the sons of one and the same mother. The father of the biggest of them (is) from the land of Egypt; as for the other who has little pity, his father (is) from the people of Judah and it is not known from which city (precisely) his father is." Finally, we remained six months dwelling at the mountain of Qosqâm, me and my Son Jesus, until Moses (Musis) came to us with the news of his (= his own) fear. Moses died and we buried him there, for he had troubled us with what had come to us with him of rumors. He (remained) buried in this place until this day. After that, we did not remain in this place but a short time. Behold the news of Gabriel the angel came to us and informed us of the disappearance of Herod. We returned to the land of Jerusalem where the Jews crucified my beloved Son.


[p. 254] {#po.49.p254}
ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ አብቁ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ልብከ ፡ ወአእምር ፡ ዘእቤለከ' ፡ ወጸሐፍ ፡ መክብቦ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ምጽአትነ ፡ ወንብረትነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። እስመ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዘአመርኩከ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ኢከሠትክዎ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ ታኦፊሎስ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ዓፅቅ ፡ ጽጉይ ፤ ከመ ፡ማርቆስ ፡ ተክል ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘኢይፈልስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
Now, Timothy, open the thought of your heart, know what I have told you and write everything in memory of our coming and our stay on this rock; indeed, what I have taught you, I have not revealed to your father Theophilus. You are a flowering branch like Mark, the new plant that will never disappear."

Chapter 19 (Arabic 139) My Lady the Virgin also made me walk through the place, she indicated to me the place where she had washed her beloved Son, the place (where his) toes (had rested) and the place where he had placed his seat so that we might build a church there. She taught me everything that would happen to us in the house of the holy abba Pachomius in the South. She said to me: "Behold he (= Pachomius?) will come to you on this day when you will consecrate the church of abba Pachomius; will come (also) to you the numerous luminous armies, many bishops and the patriarchs, (from) the first of them, Mark the evangelist, until your father Dioscorus. Will come also to you a numerous assembly of monks, at the consecration, from the holy abba Paul (Bulä) the great and abba Palamon (Palämon) until three generations of all the monks; all will come to you at the consecration of the sanctuary of the holy abba Pachomius. And he who is greater than those who will come to you, my beloved Son, will come also to you at the consecration and will officiate before you. Many tribes of the children of Israel will gather to you at the moment of the consecration. I also, I will come, my armies with me, and I will commune from the hand of my beloved Son. May the first consecration take place on the first of genbot, it (is) the day when I was born." The birth of Our Lady according to the computation of the Egyptians (is) the first of genbot and, according to the computation of (the other) half of the Christians, the tenth of maskaram. "Now, Timothy, behold I have indicated to you the day when you will consecrate the church of abba Pachomius publicly. Now, weigh well everything that I have indicated to you to accomplish it on the rock in memory of me and of my Son in this place forever." When she had said this, she hid herself from me. May her blessing be with us in the centuries of centuries.

Chapter 20 (Arabic 141) "As for the father patriarch ('said Peter"), when he saw this prodigious vision in a vision, his spirit was enraptured into the heights and he forgot everything that (is) in the world. There was with us a brother monk from the desert of Pbow embarked

in the boat, he was holy and a virgin. He had fallen asleep also with us in the place where he was because of the fatigue of the road and the vigil at night. He saw also Our Lady the Virgin Mary, she was walking, leading the patriarch and indicating to him the signs (relating to) the place so that he might accomplish them. He heard and understood everything that she had said to the patriarch. The name of this brother monk was Moses (Musis), he preceded the morning and approached the patriarch. He informed Peter the deacon (and said to him:) "Awaken the father patriarch so that I may inform him of what I have seen in the way of a holy mystery." This brother informed us of all the words that the Virgin had said to him before the patriarch awoke from his sleep. When I had heard, I Peter the deacon, this from the part of the brother monk, I awakened the father patriarch, for he was sleeping a deep sleep because of what he had seen of the mysteries of Our Lady the Virgin Mary. Immediately, he rose, made a long prayer and blessed the Lord; we went to him and were all blessed by him. This brother who had seen this mystery by the Virgin came to the father (patriarch) to be blessed by him. The patriarch said to him: "Be constant, my son, for the blessing of the pure Virgin, which you have seen and contemplated, just as it has shone on you, to me also it has been revealed." The brother made a prostration before him and said: "Forgive me, my father, for I do not deserve this grace, but it is you certainly who are worthy of all grace for you are, you, the new Joseph and behold you have walked with Our Lady the Virgin; behold you have been like James and (like) John in whose house the Virgin dwelt; behold the light of your face has shone before the Lord like the face of Moses, the greatest of the prophets; behold the grace of your fathers the patriarchs has dwelt upon you before the Lord and his mother, Our Lady the Virgin. She has given you the keys of (this) place and the stay (that is) there so that you might open it to the peoples; behold you have been like Joseph who governed Egypt - this one, behold he has perished and, (if) his name has not perished, his granaries and his foundries (9) have perished. But as for you, the foundation that you have founded on this rock, as it was ordered to you, will never pass. Indeed, it (is) founded on the rock, at the root of Judah which has made its blessing spring forth from the house of David." When this brother monk had said this, he withdrew from before the father patriarch.
በረከቶ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፤ ዝክቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ሰሰለ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት። ወይቤ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሶቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ወንጌሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምአፈ” ፡ ፪ወ፻ ሰማዕት ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ቃል ። ሠናይ ፡ ስም ፅከ ፡ ዘትቤ ፤ ኦእኀ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ እቤሎ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳ ሳት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይቤለኒ ፡ ሊተ” ፡ ዝኩ ፡ እጐ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወጠንቀቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጐ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ አብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ምስሌከኑ ፡ ረፈቀ ። ወእቤሎ ፡ እወ ።
The patriarch said at that moment: "Behold our Lord has said in his holy gospel: 'By the mouth of two or three witnesses is established every word.' Beautiful (is) your testimony that you have said, brother." And I, Peter the deacon, I retold to my father the patriarch everything that this brother had told me and we measured exactly that the brother (was) holy. The father patriarch said to me

again: "Have you...?" I said to him: "Yes." Immediately, the patriarch dictated (to me) everything that Our Lady the Virgin had told him. He wrote also to the governor of Antinoe ('Ensenä) - his name was Aladion ('Alädeyun) - so that he might send us stone cutters and masons for the organization of the place of the construction of the sanctuary. We remained on this rock seven days waiting for the commander who had been sent with us, for he had not embarked with us on the boat from Alexandria. Chapter 21 (Arabic 143) When the message of the patriarch arrived to the governor of Antinoe (saying) that the patriarch abba Timothy was calling him to a spiritual work, (the governor) did not hesitate then, but he hastened immediately and came to us with all his retinue and his army; they were blessed by the father (patriarch). The father patriarch told him everything that he had seen, the account of the mystery that Our Lady the Virgin had made to him. He (= the governor) rejoiced, exulted and prostrated himself before the Lord. He dispatched immediately to his city and brought masons, stone cutters and all the tools with them. He offered also to the father patriarch much money. While we were there on the rock, the news (concerning) the father patriarch reached all the region, namely that the patriarch, the father abba Timothy, (was) in this place. Many bishops came to us to be blessed by the patriarch. They were going with him to take care of the consecration of the sanctuary of the holy abba Pachomius of Pbow, according to the command of the emperor. Came also to us Basil (Bäselyos), bishop of Taha (Teha) with an idle and very arrogant splendor; with him (were) arrogant people. He was not like all the (other) bishops, but he was like a governor or a commander. His appearance was (exaggeratedly) in evidence in the midst of the servants who (were) with him. This one came (therefore) and, with him, someone who had much (in the way of gifts). When I saw him, I Peter, I recognized him and said to the patriarch: "Do you not see the boastfulness of this bishop and his pride? This was also his way of acting in the days of the patriarch during his exile and he did not unite with us in this trial. This other one also, that is to say George (Giyorgis) the idolater who (is) in the city of Adriga ('Adrigä) (is) likewise." When I had said this to my father, he recognized this bishop also. He said to me: "Peter, behold you (have recognized him well or do you not know that he has wept, has been afflicted and (has regretted?) the shameful action that I had told him (to regret) while my father Dioscorus was sitting beside me? This thing (is) manifest. Many


[p. 260] {#po.49.p260}
ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳተ' ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ይገብኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ድኅር ፡፤ ወይበክዩ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኃጣውኢሆም ፤እስከ ፡ ዕለትነ ፡ ዝንቱ ። ወኢተአምርኑ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ዘበጻሕነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዕልዋን ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤነ ፡ አንስጣስዮስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆቆፅስ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አናብዕ ፡ ወግዒር ፡ ወተአመነ ፡ ኃጢአቶ ፡ ወፈታሕናሁ ፡ ወሖረ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ተናገርኩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በዛህን ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተኬ ፡ ዘድልው ፡ ለክብራት ፣ ፈድፋዶደ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ወአእላፈ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ምንትኒ ፡ እምሥጢራት ፡ ዘኅቡእ ፡ እምኔከ ።
bishops of the land of Egypt return backward and weep because of their sins until this day (which is) ours. Do you not know the day when we arrived at the heretics'? Anastasius ('Anestäseyos) the bishop came to us, he poured abundant tears with weeping and groaning, he confessed his sin, we absolved him and he went away." I, Peter the deacon, when my father had said this word to me, I spoke with him quietly and said to him: "Forgive me, my father, it is certainly you who are worthy of honors, more than me by many thousands of times; there is no mystery that (is) hidden from you."

Chapter 22 (Arabic 145) In that night when the patriarch had seen clearly (?) this in the soul of these bishops, it was revealed to him in a vision what they deserved in the way of excommunication, prohibition and deposition. When he awoke from his sleep, he said to me: "The bishops whom I have cast out by my mouth, behold the Holy Spirit also has cast them out from this place and from among the bishops." In this hour, (the patriarch) saw the father Dioscorus and abba Macarius ('Abumagaär); they had come in spirit to visit us, bishops and patriarchs (were) with them. They went with us as if they had been called to the consecration of the church of abba Pachomius of the desert of Pbow; they all came and prostrated themselves before God on this rock while glorifying God on this rock. Then those who were the chiefs of the bishops turned toward the father Dioscorus and said to him: "Dioscorus, Dioscorus, the branch that (is) yours, behold it has sprouted and produced; it has become a memory of you forever (and) for always. Behold indeed that Timothy has planted the vineyard of the Lord in this place. Behold he has put juice of his vineyard in the cup of the Lord, for he is your son, yours and also ours forever, for always and in the centuries of centuries. Now, order that Basil be cut off, cast out from the holy mysteries and from the episcopal dignity, for he has united with Leo the heretic in his faith and has insulted Dioscorus with his tongue and likewise abba Macarius, the bishop of Tkôou (Fäw). This one (= Basil) has given much money to the envoy of the emperor with whom was a letter (from him), he has turned many bishops away from going to Dioscorus. The sin of the people of his see will certainly come upon him, his city will be ruined and his land will be taken into captivity. Likewise, this one, that is to say George the impious, has blasphemed Our Lady the Virgin who is Our Lady raised above everything that (is) in the heavens and (on) the earth." (Dioscorus said to Timothy:) "Now, my son Timothy, when you have risen, cast out these two (men) publicly." "When my father (said"

After Timothy had spoken to me in this vision, I awoke. His words were sweet to my ear as he comforted me, he and his fathers the patriarchs. 'Now, Peter,' he continued, 'behold, I know that you are a Christian of the right faith. Now, my son, rise and prepare an altar for us so that we may commune here today, we and the commander with whom is all this assembly, for it is Saturday.' I, Peter, said to the father patriarch: 'How shall we commune here when no church has been built here until now?' My father gave me an order, saying: 'Make an altar in this place and begin before the construction of the place around the throne.' I did so and we performed the celebration. This is the first Eucharistic offering that was made in this place upon this rock. The day was the fourteenth of the month of Tegemt. The whole assembly of gathered people came to commune first. Then he (= the patriarch) cried out, saying: 'Basil, Basil who is not worthy of the episcopal office and is separated and anathematized, and likewise George the impious, they are anathematized, outside the episcopate and removed from the grace of the Holy Spirit! They have no part in the hierarchy of the episcopate, neither here nor in the final place, forever.' The patriarch further ordered the governor of Antinoe to strip them, to tear their garments, to expel them from their cities, and to transfer to the empire whatever goods they had.

Chapter 23 (Arabic 147) As for those cursed heretics whom we have mentioned, they stirred up against us the people of the see of Taha and they said: 'Who appointed you over us as leader again and patriarch?' When they did this against us, we pronounced upon them the word of curse. When it was the next day, the commander who was with us came. He arrived at the see of Taha, to those who had stirred up a dispute against us. We informed him of this and also of what had happened because of them. He surrounded them, he and his soldiers; he burned half of them, he killed half of them by the sword. He seized all their goods, he destroyed their city and rendered it deserted and desolate. As for this Basil the heretical bishop, he sent him into the dungeon of Antinoe and he died an evil death. After this trouble that had occurred had subsided, the commander who was with us questioned the father patriarch and said to him: 'Why do we remain in this place? We were sent for the consecration of the church of Abba Pachomius. As for what we have done here on this rock, it is without the consent of the emperor.' The patriarch Abba Timothy recounted to the commander everything that Our Lady the Virgin Mary had shown him regarding this place. The commander went with him and (the patriarch) showed him that Our Lady the Virgin Mary had shown him all this and what the Lord Christ had done in his childhood, and his (= Christ's) hand which he had imprinted

on this rock, and his prohibition made to this rock to follow him from there. But the commander thought in his heart and his soul was weakened. He doubted within himself and said: 'Does this action come from human nature?' There was a servant standing before him whose name was Ammonios. He cried out, saying: 'O commander, do not doubt and do not think with suspicion of this thing; it is not something that happened in this region alone: behold, there is also a stele that the Son of God erected in a region by the river in memory of his coming in his childhood. All the people upon whom is an illness coming from all evils, when they prostrate themselves before it, they are healed. Now, my lord, truly no thing is impossible to God; all the mountains and rocks that he created obey him and fear him more than men.' When Ammonios had said this to him (= the commander), the assembly hearing it, behold, a wonder from heaven appeared, a star descending. It appeared by day and all the gathered people saw it in broad daylight, in the likeness of marble and a precious stone. It stood to the right of the Lord at the place of the rock. It remained there, its lower part being like a precious stone. The commander grasped it in his hand, all the people seeing him. All cried out, saying: 'Kyrie eleison, one is God and one is the orthodox faith.' As for the commander, he rejoiced at what he had seen. He dispatched to the emperor, sent him the blessing coming from this place, and informed him of the history of this place. He ordered the stonecutters to cut a piece of this rock, and they began to cut. The stone that the quarrymen were working on softened for them like water. Who then can recount what happened at that moment! This star that the people had seen also became like flowing water, and these two wonders appeared at one and the same moment, so that those who had not believed in what had happened previously and in what the Lord had done in the way of miracles in Egypt, believed in what had happened.

Chapter 24 (Arabic 151) There was a notable among the people of Alexandria - he had boarded the boat with us - whose name was Anatolius. An unclean spirit was in him and this unclean spirit had made him fall according to his habit. We were astonished at the greatness of this man and at what this spirit was doing in him. As for me, I ordered those who were there to make him (= the man) go out toward the rock upon which the right hand of the Lord was imprinted. This spirit cried out, saying: 'I adjure you, by Jesus the Nazarene, not to make me go out of

ከመ ፡ ኢታውፅአኒ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወለእመ ፡ አውፃእከኒ ፡ እምኔሁ' ፡ ኢትፈንወኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማዕምቅት ፡ ኅበ ፡ዘዘፈነውካ ፡ ለእንታክቲ ፡ በዓልተ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ እንተ ፡ አብጻሕካ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማዕምቅት ። እስመ ፡፣ በፈቃድከ ፡ ኀደርኩ ፡ አነ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ መግደላዊትኒ ፡ ወበትእዛ ዝከ ፡ ኀደርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱኒ ፡ ብእሲ ። ወናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ምሙዕ" ፡ ወፍሩሀ'! ፡ እም ኔከ ። "ወአነ ፡ እወፅእ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢይገብእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፍርሃተ ፡ ፈራህኩ ፡ እምየማንከ ፤ ዘጥዑቅ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ገብአ ፡ ልቡ ፡፣ ለዝኩ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወሐይወ ፡፣ እምዘኮነ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ዝኩኒ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሰረረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ ኅፉረ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዛተ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአመሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። ወነሥአ ፡ እምነ ፡ ግማደ ፡ አእባን ፣ ዘገመድዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ መካን ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጽሕፈት ። ንሕነሰ ፡ ተንሣእነ ፡ ወተፈኖነ ፡ ፅሩጋውያነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መርጡሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቡጳኩም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ያሜንዩ ፡ መካነ ፡ ሠናየ ። ወኀደግናሆሙ ፡ ለፀረብተ ፡ እብን ፡ ይግምዱ ፡፤ አእባነ ። ወደመርነ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ጸደ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቢር ። ወእንዘ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ኩልነ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ንሬኢ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መልዕልተ ፤ ኩዙኩሕ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ቤዝ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ቀንዲል ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐቱ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ፳8ይሬኢ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘይትጌበር ፡ ወፀረብተ ፡ እብን ፡ ይትጌበሩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወጸራሕነ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንብል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ መንክራተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይገብሮን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሙላዲሁ ። ወናሁ ፡ኮነ፣ ቤዙ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ኃጥአን ፡ እስከ ፣፡ መጽኡ ፡ ወአብኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡፤ ዮሩርባናቲ ሆሙ ። ወናሁመ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ ዛተ ፡ መንክረ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቤዝ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ኢኀደገ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ለነ ። ወንሕነ ፡ ንሬእዮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕርበተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወተደመረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብያዝ ፡ እለ ፡ ይወፅኡ ።
him. And if you make me go out of him, do not send me into the abyss where you sent that sorceress whom you caused to reach the abyss. Indeed, it is by your will that I have dwelt in (Mary) Magdalene and it is by your order that I have dwelt in this man also. Behold, I am terrified and frightened by you. I will go out of him and I will never return to him, for I have great fear of your right hand which is imprinted in this place.' After an hour, the heart of this man returned to him and he was healed of that in which he had been. As for this spirit that had gone out of him, it flew away like the wind and fled ashamed. When the commander saw this wonder, he wrote to the emperor and informed him of everything that had occurred on this rock. He took pieces of the stones that had been broken off while building the place and sent them to the emperor with a letter. As for us, we arose and were sent, going up toward the sanctuary of the holy Abba Pachomius for its consecration. We would be absent until the moment when one would have beautifully finished the arrangement of the place. We left the stonecutters so that they might break off the stones. We added one of the commander's soldiers to them in the work. While we were all in the boat and were looking toward the top of the rock, behold, a star appeared again in the air, in the likeness of a burning lamp. Everyone was looking at it, it was above the place that was being worked; the stonecutters were working there. We all cried out, saying: 'Holy, holy are the wonders of God that he performs in his place of birth. Behold, his star had appeared to sinners so that they came and brought him their offerings. And behold precisely that, at the moment of his coming to Egypt, we have seen this wonder.' This star that shone like a torch, it did not cease to shine for us: we saw it until the setting of the sun and it joined the stars that were rising.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኃለፍነ ፤ ዝክተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ወበጻሕነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መርጡሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቡጳኩም ። ወርኢነ ፡ መስተገብራን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጌበሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትጌበሩ ፡ ውእቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታፅካተ" ፡ ነገሥት ። ወርኢነ ፡ ውሉዶ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ በአምሳሊሆሙ ፡ ለመላእክት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጻሕነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወሠርዑ ፡ ፪ መናብርተ ፡፣ በጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ። ወናሁ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ለነ ፡ በዝኩ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወውእ ቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሊቀ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወእግዝአትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ መርት" ፡ ማርያም ፡ አስተርአዩ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ኢትሚም ፡ እሎንተ ፡ ፪ አእባነ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አሐቲ፣ ተቅዋመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኢመፍትው ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ተቅዋመ ፡ ማኅ ቶት ፡ ሚም ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ እሎንተ ፡ ፪ አአባነ ፡ ክቡራነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያኅትዉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ፣
After having crossed this place, we arrived at the sanctuary of the holy Abba Pachomius. We saw the workers working as if they were working on the palaces of kings. We saw his (= Pachomius's) sons the monks, they were in the likeness of angels. When we arrived at the moment of the consecration, many of the children of Israel gathered around us; they arranged four seats at the moment of the consecration. Behold, a great sign and an immense glory appeared to us at that moment. This happened thus: the archangel Michael and Our Lady the Virgin Dame Mary appeared to the father patriarch Abba Timothy. Our Lady the Virgin Mary said to him: 'Timothy, do not place these two stones on a single candelabrum, for that is not fitting, but place on this candelabrum these two precious stones that are here so that they may shine: indeed, this light is for Michael the archangel; as for the second light, it is for the holy Abba Pachomius, the father of this desert. This too will never disappear from this place. Timothy,'


[p. 268] {#po.49.p268}
ብርሃን ፡ ይከውን ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡፣ መላእክት ። ወካልእኒ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ አቡጳኩም ፡ አበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወዝካዕበ ፡ ኢይሴስል ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወናሁ ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ተዕካከኒ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ወብርሃነ ፡ ሚካኤልኒ ፡ ኢየኃልፍ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወዋካ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወንስሐ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ ወፍሥሐ ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኢትቀድስ ፡ ዛተ ፡ መርጠለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፲ ወ ፳ ለኅዳር ፡ እስመ ፡ ነዋ ፡ ደደቀት" ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እምኩሎን ፡ መካናት ። ወይመጽእ ፡ ወልድየኒ ፡፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዝየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ ዘንተ" ፣ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ተከብተት ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀ ፡ አብ ፡ እምን ዋሙ ፡ አመረነ ፡ ዘርእዮ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ወዘትቤሎ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማር
behold, the light of your palace also will shine thus and the light of Michael also will never pass away. Indeed, the light of the Father and the radiance of the Son are a power in this place, a penance for sinners and a joy and a grace for the Holy Spirit. Now, do not consecrate this sanctuary before the fifteenth of Hedar, for behold, the coming of the saints from all places will occur. My Son, the great king Jesus Christ, will also come here.' After Our Lady the Virgin Mary had said this to him, she vanished from his sight. When the father awoke from his sleep, he informed us of what he had seen in this vision and what Our Lady the Virgin Mary had said to him. Behold, we accomplished all the will of the great God, we consecrated the sanctuary publicly and remained in this place for three months.

Chapter 25 (Arabic 153) And again, after that, we returned and descended the river to occupy ourselves with the place of Our Lady the Virgin Mary that we had decided to establish firmly on this rock. When we arrived to them (= those who had been left), we found that behold, they had finished this work with the help of God, and we found the rescript of the emperor addressed to the commander who was with us. Behold, it had arrived to him accompanied by silver coins. The emperor had sent them to him for the construction that was necessary and to maintain the brothers who would remain in this place. The emperor informed us in his letter that the piece of stone that had been sent to him had healed all the sick who were in the city so that they had said: 'These beautiful branches were cut in paradise.' We consecrated the church on the twenty-first of the month of Terr, which is the day of the death of Our Lady the Virgin Mary, just as it had been revealed to us. 'Behold, I, Timothy,' said the patriarch, 'am going to speak and God is my witness that I looked toward the top of the dome to distinguish the construction that was made in the church - behold, this church is small - and I saw this star that I had seen before, I saw it a second time.' When it was the next day, Paphnutius, the disciple of the holy Abba Macarius whom my father consecrated bishop, saw a vision. He said to my father: 'I saw this night an assembly of many people lamenting and weeping and as if they had been saved from a shipwreck. There were also other people, they had drowned in the river. There was also a tall man, he came to you and informed you of their history; you, you were at the moment of the Eucharist.' When Paphnutius had said this to him, Timothy was grieved. Paphnutius said to him again: 'I saw the Virgin, the Lady, and in her hand there was a golden staff. Behold, she'

was carrying the souls of those who had been shipwrecked, she brought them to you, saying: "Timothy, these who died because of me, behold, I have taken them and brought them to you so that you might commune them before the living. As for those who came for a matter of commerce, God will condemn them."' When Paphnutius had said this, we waited for the fulfillment of his words. Believe us then, brothers and all the generations that will come to this place: while we were standing in this place, behold, the vision of Paphnutius was fulfilled! When it was Sunday - we were at the moment of the Eucharist -, behold, a boat came and was shipwrecked; those who were in it died. Father bishops have confessed to us that they saw the souls of those who had died, behold, they communed before the living. From that day, my father knew with certainty that the spirit of grace that was in the holy Abba Macarius dwelt in Paphnutius his disciple. Chapter 26 (Arabic 155) One day, a woman took a bottle of wine and a jug of oil. She wanted to come to us with them. Her donkey stumbled, the vessels in which were the wine and the oil broke and nothing of them remained in the jugs, for she did not find other jugs. She came weeping and informed the father of her story. 'I asked Our Lady the Virgin Mary,' said the patriarch, 'to show me the secret of the woman. This was revealed to me and I saw Our Lady the Virgin Mary saying to me: "O patriarch, when you have come to the place of the Eucharistic sacrifice, look to your side; behold, you will see a man and, in his hand, there will be a vial of wine. Take it from him, for it is the wine that was with this woman, and commune the people with it according to what I have told you."' When it was the next day, the father entered the altar and saw the angel of the Lord near him, a bottle of wine in his hand; the angel presented it to the father. The father said to him: 'Who are you, you who are in this holy place?' The angel answered, saying: 'I am the guardian angel of all the father patriarchs since the moment when Cyril consecrated Dioscorus your father, I am his guardian and I have never abandoned him even for a single hour. In his turn, Dioscorus entrusted you to me, Timothy, I have never separated from you and I will serve you at the altar of God forever. Now, it is in truth the Lady, the Virgin and Queen Mary who ordered me to take from this woman the vessel of wine and the vessel of oil that broke and to bring them here. Now, take this wine and give communion with it; it will be a sign for this widow woman. Behold also the bottle of oil, nothing of it was lost; on the contrary, the oil is in its bottle according to its usual manner of being. Behold, the Lady washed

her Son with it when they were on this rock the day they arrived at it during their coming to Egypt.' Wonder at the immensity of the clamor that arose at that moment from the assembly of the gathered people! They were pressed one against the other and were looking at the jug of oil, this one catching fire, boiling, and spreading among them. Wonder at the immensity of the splendors, miracles, and wonders that occurred from this oil at that moment! 'While we were in this place,' said the patriarch, 'behold, the captain of the boat that was with us came to me and said: "My lord the patriarch, we want to leave here, for long days have passed for us, and much time, I and my boat being inactive."' My father, looking at him, said to him: 'My son, why do you say that behold, you are weary of inactivity? Go in peace.' He gave him his wages and the captain went away.

Chapter 27 (Arabic 157) Three days after his departure, Abba Baberleyas, bishop of Syria, came to us. Indeed, behold, he had come to the city of Alexandria. He questioned peaceful people and they informed him about us that we were in the land of Egypt. He came to us and informed us, saying: 'The boat that was with you was shipwrecked and all those who were in it perished.' As for the father Timothy, he did not know the place from where Baberleyas came, for he was not with us at the moment of... When he saw him, the spirit of grace moved him, the secret appeared to him, and this Baberleyas who had informed the patriarch said that he had saved himself from a wolf and a destructive animal. The father Abba Timothy said to him: 'Tell me why you visited the patriarch and how you came to us today.' Baberleyas answered and said to him: 'Yes in truth, my father, there is nothing that is hidden from you. Behold, I am going to inform you of what God has done to the enemies of the Church.' He informed us of the death of Juvenal in Jerusalem and of Leontius, and he informed us that they had not deserved that their bodies be buried. As for the blacksmith, his body and all his entrails had rotted; he had died and they had thrown him into the sea. As for the other, he had been cut to pieces entirely and had died, no one had been able to touch him. The father told him completely how it had happened: everything was accomplished thus. 'Behold,' continued the bishop, 'that I have been safe and that I have come to you to see if I would be comforted by your sight.' Baberleyas, bishop of Syria, remained six days with us and died. He was the first who

was buried in this place.' 'He asked me,' said Timothy, 'that his name be mentioned with the number of the other dead in memory of his coming to us. This is what I have told you: glory to God and to Our Lady the Virgin Mary who does not depart from me in all the days of my life!'

Chapter 28 (Arabic 159) While we were in this place, the thought came to me, your father Timothy, to ordain this old man Abba Moses as priest and minister of the church, for he was the origin of this holy place. He knew this by the spirit and hid himself from us. We were looking for the old man in this place and we did not find him. On the other hand, the angel of the Lord appeared to us in the aspect of a man. He came to us while the crowd was assembled, he was singing and saying: 'One is the God of this pure and solitary old man, behold, he is in this cave under this rock.' Behold, I entered to him and I found him speaking with the angel, he was weeping, saying: 'I am not worthy of what is wanted to be done to me.' I heard the angel saying to him: 'There will be no cessation until the patriarch ordains you priest today and he who will come after him will consecrate you bishop.' When the angel had said this to me, we said to him: 'Do you know his dwelling, for we have not found it?' He showed us his dwelling, saying: 'Behold, he is under the rock.' As for me, I sent Peter the deacon as well as other brothers to him. They brought him to me - he was weeping -, I said to him: 'Do not weep, O man of God, for this action comes from God.' Immediately, we prepared the consecration of the Eucharist and we ordained this old man priest. Peter the deacon and Paphnutius the bishop approached for the consecration and performed it, for the commander who was with us had pressed us to set out toward the emperor. When we arrived at the consecration, I heard a beautiful voice come out of Peter saying: 'Holy, holy, holy!' At the same moment, Peter fell on his face and I saw that behold, the face of Paphnutius shone like that of Abba Macarius, it shone more, and behold, he became afraid. I heard another voice saying: 'Behold, he has come, the new Melchizedek who is the image of the Lord Christ in the flesh.' I saw at this hour a dome of light, behold, it had descended from heaven: this entire mountain shone like the radiance of the sun by the light of this tent. Christ is my witness that I do not say this object of glory for myself, but because of the mystery of the Lord Christ, for he is one, he, his excellent Father, and the Holy Spirit in the ages of ages, amen.

Chapter 29 (Arabic 161) What came out of the luminous tent was the lamb of God bearing the sins of the world. I saw, I who do not deserve it, Timothy, the Lord Christ breaking the bread alone above the altar and consecrating and signing the blood. Behold, I heard him saying: 'Let Stephen come to his service to carry the chalice.' At this moment, Peter the deacon fell again. Then I heard the service of Stephen, he was singing hymns with joy. O Stephen, what a great and very majestic man! I saw also two other men inclining the chalice, saying: 'Abareskaman.' I saw the Lord Christ, behold, he stretched out his finger, he put it into the chalice while performing the consecration and he took of the blood. I heard many people glorifying the Lord Christ so that I said: 'The dead have risen from their tombs,' because of the number of voices. I myself collapsed and could not rise, but God did not abandon me in my collapsed position. The eyes of my soul saw the vision of my Lord and there occurred glorifications that surrounded him while he was signing the chalice and blessing it three times, saying: 'Holy, holy, holy are the Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit! The Christ who became incarnate of the Virgin Mary, this is his body and his blood, amen.' I heard also a second invocation, it said this glorification: 'Holy is he who took the aspect of a man, holy is he who came with humility while he is in the high heavens forever, holy is he on whose chest John lay, holy is the Christ! This is his body and his blood, amen.' I heard also a third invocation saying over the chalice: 'Holy is the hand of the Father, holy is he who springs from the heart of the Father, holy is the Son of the Virgin Mary, holy is the friend of the apostles, holy is Jesus Christ! This is his body and his blood, amen.' I heard this third invocation with which was the chalice, it said also: 'May the grace of Christ be for all the believers who receive of them (= the holy species).' Immediately, Our Lady the Virgin Mary appeared in a great glory. I heard the voice of the Lord Christ saying to her: 'Come to my right, O my pure mother, the pure bride, my pure dwelling, the perfect virgin.' I saw his miraculous body and the blood of the chalice in his hands and he gave communion with it. He began to commune his mother the Virgin and Our Lady in her turn did this. She turned again toward me while I was still lying and she said to me: 'Timothy, open your eyes and listen with your ears to the glorification with which my beloved Son glorifies me with his divine mouth. Now, write this in memory of me and everything that you have seen on this rock. Be strong and courageous, for

ወጽናዕ ፡ ወተኅየል ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘኀደርኩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፣፤ እምቅድመ ፣ እሑር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቀስቋም ። ወጸሐፍ ፡ ካፅበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ 'ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ዛተ፡!፣ ገድልየ ፡ ትሑተ ፡ ዘተወከፋ” ፡ ኢይሬኢ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካነ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም” ። ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ዛቲ፣ ኩኩሕ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀደርኩ” ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አነ ፡ ወወልድየ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ስደትነ ። ወጸሐፍ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘያስተሐምም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽሒፈ ፡ ተዝካርየ ፡ ወገድ ልየ ፡ አነኒ ፤ አስተሐምም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ራት ፡ እንተ ፣ ኢተኃልፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። አነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢይኩን ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘኢየአምን ፡ ድካማትየ ፡ ወኢድካመ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ወኢሞትየ ፡ ወኢልደትየ ፡ እምአበ ውየ ። ወሠናይኬ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢተወልደ ፡ ፍጹመ” ፡ ውስተ፣፤ ዓለም” ፡ እስመ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ" ፡ ሥቃየ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ተውህበኒ ፤ ሥልጣን ፡ ከመ ፡ እምሐሮሙ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሞትየ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ ወ፳ ለወርኃ ፡ ጥር ። ወአእምር ፡ ኦጢሞቴዎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ዘርኢካሁ ፡ ዮም ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ናሁኬ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ቀዶሶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ቅድሜከ ። ወአነ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ እምድንግል ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ ወቅድስት” ፡ ማርያም ። ወካዕበ ፡ ጐድአተኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቦየ ፡ ዘኮንኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ውዱቀ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፤ ወተንሣአኩ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፤ ፍሥሕየ ፥ ወኅሠይየ” ፣ እንዘ ፣ እሴብሖ” ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአአኩቶ'፤ ፡ ወለወላዲቱ” ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘረሰየተኒ ፡ ድልወ ፣ ለዘርኢክዎ ፡ እመንክራት ። ወናሁኬ ፡ አነ ፡ እከውን ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘርኢክዎ ፡ አማን ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአነ ፡ አአምን ፡ እስከ ፡ ነፍስ” ፡ ደኃሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ከሠተት ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሙሲስ ፡ ቀሚሥ ፡ ዘሜምክዎ ፡ ዮም ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ በአስተብቀሞዖፆታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ "ንጽሕት ፡ ወድንግልት” ፡ ማርያም ፡ ከመ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ወልዳ”፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡
this is the place where I dwelt before going to the mountain of Qosqam. Write also that whoever writes this humble life of mine and receives it will never see the place of hell. Timothy, if there is a man who believes everything that happened to us on this rock upon which I dwelt, I and my beloved Son, at the time of our persecution... Write also that for whoever will take care to write my memorial and my life, I will take care of his soul with goods that will never pass away. I am Mary the Virgin, let there be no man who does not believe in my trials and in the trial of my Son, in my death and in my birth from my parents. It would certainly be better for this man that he had never been born into this world, for for all those who are in all the torments of hell, it has been given to me power to have mercy on them, especially the day of my death which is the twenty-first of the month of Terr. Know, Timothy, by this vision that you have seen today in this place, that behold, my Son has consecrated this place before you.' I, Timothy, heard this from the pure and holy Virgin Mary. Then she struck my side on which I had fallen, and I rose immediately, joyful and exultant, glorifying God and praising him as well as his mother the Virgin Mary who had rendered me worthy to see what I had seen in the way of miracles. Behold, I am a witness for you that everything I have seen is true. I will believe until my last breath that Our Lady the Virgin Mary revealed all this to me from the holy Abba Moses the priest whom I ordained today as priest by the intercession of Our Lady the pure and virgin Mary so that we might find mercy before her Son Jesus Christ our Lord, to whom belong all glory and all honor as well as to his excellent Father and to the Holy Spirit, henceforth, until the end of all time and forever, amen and amen.
"ወኩሉ ፡ ክብር ፡ ለአብ ፡ ጌር ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ጊዜያት ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን” ። መልአ ፡ ድርሳነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፡፤ ወወላዲቱ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኅድረቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ደብር ፡ በሰላም ፡ ዘእምእግዚአብሔር ፡ አሜን ።
Finished is the homily of the holy Timothy which is about the coming of our Lord Jesus Christ and his mother Our Lady Mary into the land of Egypt and about their stay on the rock of the mountain, in the peace of God, amen.
